# Composer harness — findings log

Append-only. One row per defect the harness surfaced. Newest last.

`Real?` is `confirmed` once the defect has been reproduced in the Telegram app itself,
`harness-only` once it has been shown not to reproduce there, and `provisional` until someone has
checked. **Height and inset findings start `provisional` by rule** — the harness does not have
`ChatControllerNode`'s keyboard machinery.

| Date | Symptom | Tier | Test | Real? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: tapping into the composer raised the system "Allow Paste" prompt whenever the clipboard held another app's content, and the prompt took the keyboard down. `canPerformAction(paste:)` asked `canPasteMedia`, which was `handlePastedMedia(perform: false)` — it READ the clipboard (gif/mp4/heics data, then `items`). **Fixed:** `canPasteMedia` answers from `ChatTextInputPanelNode.pasteboardMayHoldMedia()` (`hasImages` + `contains(pasteboardTypes:)`, no read). The first Tier-2 test could not fail cleanly (the app's main thread blocks in the read while the prompt is up, so XCUITest cannot snapshot it) and was reduced to Tier 1 with a pasteboard-read spy. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessPastePromptTests.swift` — `HarnessPastePromptTests` (red before the fix: 4 reads) | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: losing focus reset the selection to `0..<0`. **Not reproduced** through `ensureUnfocused()` nor through another window becoming key; only ever seen after the paste prompt (above) took focus. Tests removed; closed. | — | — | not reproduced |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: Backspace over a selection deleted it but left Cut / Copy / Paste / Format on screen. `TGIDObservingBlockTextView.deleteBackward` routes a selection deletion through `replaceRange:withText:` (for the structural gate), which is not where UIKit hides the menu. **Fixed:** dismiss the view's `UIEditMenuInteraction` after that replacement. | 2 | `UITests/HarnessEditMenuUITests.swift` — `testBackspaceOverSelectAllDismissesTheEditMenu` (red before the fix) | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: the first, focusing tap at the end of the text raised Paste / Select / Select All (K1: 3/5 and 4/10 engine, 0/5 legacy). Traced: the panel's `TouchDownGestureRecognizer` called `ensureFocusedOnTap()` from `touchesEnded`, so UIKit's text multi-tap action found the engine ALREADY first responder with its caret under the finger and read the tap as a tap on the caret. Deferring one run-loop turn was not enough (5/5 → 1/5 in the test, 4/10 on K1): the multi-tap action arrives in a later event. **Fixed:** for the engine the panel no longer focuses on a tap; UIKit's text tap does it (traced: `_handleMultiTapGesture:` now enters with `fr=false`), with a 0.3 s fallback for a tap the editor did not take. K1 after: 0/10 menus, keyboard 10/10. The first version of the test passed because its tap (dx 0.95) landed on the send button, which sits inside the text view's frame. | 2 | `UITests/HarnessEditMenuUITests.swift` — `testTheFocusingTapDoesNotRaiseTheEditMenu` (red before the fix: 5/5) | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: inside a CODE BLOCK the keyboard still autocapitalizes, autocorrects and spell-checks — typing `let teh ` on a code line gave `Let the `. The engine view reports fixed prose traits (`IDBlockTextView.m:168-171`; lldb with the caret in code: autocapitalization 2, autocorrection/spell-checking default); the previous backend switched them off for `caretIsInCodeRegion` and reloaded the keyboard on entering/leaving code. Legacy has no code blocks. Seen twice on K1. **Fixed:** per-region traits in `RichTextEngineEditorView+CodeInputTraits.swift` (code body and language line off; refreshed on selection change, after settle, on document set); the engine's `setSpellCheckingType:` mapped `.default` to off and now restores the controller's own setting; `id_updateTextInputTraits:` reloads the keyboard at most once, only on a real change. **Extended 2026-09-23:** smart quotes, smart dashes and smart insert/delete are now also off in code (`testACaretInACodeBlockTurnsOffSmartPunctuation`, red first): the first fix had left `"` → curly quote in code. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessCodeBlockInputTraitsTests.swift` — `HarnessCodeBlockInputTraitsTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: a selection inside a multi-line QUOTE / PULL QUOTE / CODE block is drawn as one rect as tall as the whole block (e.g. "lazy" on line 2 of a quote highlights lines 1-2; `selectionRectsForRange:` gives h=47/52 where a plain paragraph gives 23). In a pull quote a multi-line selection's rects also start at the box/composer edge, so the highlight spills outside the centred box. Plain paragraphs are correct (control test included). **Fixed:** `IDDocumentLayoutCoordinator.m` added the container box's top/bottom padding to any segment that was merely first/last WITHIN the block; now only when the selection covers the block's start/end. The multi-line pull-quote selection that extended to the column/editor edges was fixed 2026-09-23: a covered side of a block WITH its own box now takes the box edge exactly (TextKit's own line fill reaches past a pull quote's pill); box-less blocks keep the growth-only rule (`HarnessPullQuoteSelectionColumnTests`, red first). | 1 | `Tests/HarnessContainerSelectionRectTests.swift` — `HarnessContainerSelectionRectTests` (the plain-paragraph case is a passing control) | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: undoing an inline format toggle (Format ▸ Bold / Italic) drops the selection to the seed caret (44..<44 instead of 4..<9), and REDO does not re-apply the format (the step runs, `canUndo` flips back, text stays plain). Structural commands (Code) and typing round-trip fine. Seen 3× on K1 via the first responder's `undoManager` (shake/⌘Z cannot be delivered to K1). Legacy not usable as control (harness gap below). **Fixed:** one toggle registered TWO undo entries (the funnel's step with a stale snapshot selection, plus `IDInlineTextFormattingController`'s own). `recordingInlineFormatStep(selection:_:)` in `+UndoStack.swift` now registers exactly one snapshot step with the live selection; also applied to inline code, spoiler, links and the table structural-selection path, which share `transformInlineAttributes`. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessInlineFormatUndoTests.swift` — `HarnessInlineFormatUndoTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: Backspace at the start of a PULL QUOTE that follows a paragraph merges it into that paragraph, turns the pull quote's italic presentation into real `italic` runs in the model, and leaves an empty paragraph behind (`[p "First paragraph." + i"Second paragraph."] [p ""]`). Quote and code unwrap to plain paragraphs on the same key. At document start the same Backspace does nothing. Seen twice on K1. **Fixed:** new `pullQuoteStart` rule in `backspaceJoinRules` (`applyBackspaceAtStartOfPullQuote` in `+Containers.swift`) unwraps the body into body paragraphs (and keeps a non-empty author as its own paragraph), rebuilt from the model so no presentation italic carries over. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessPullQuoteBackspaceTests.swift` — `HarnessPullQuoteBackspaceTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: undoing a text edit restores the caret where the PREVIOUS edit left it, not where the undone edit happened. `…lazy dog.` type "A" at 44 → move caret to 4 → type "X" → undo: text right, selection 45..<45 (legacy: 4..<4). Same for Return after a caret move (undo to 11/17 instead of 20/30/34). The undo step restores `lastSettledUndoSnapshot`, whose selection is refreshed by settled EDITS only, never by a caret move (`+UndoStack.swift`). Seen 3× on K1; legacy control correct. **Fixed:** `refreshUndoBaselineSelection()` (`+UndoStack.swift`), called on a caret move with no edit in flight, keeps the undo baseline's selection live and ends the open typing run when the caret leaves it (a stock UITextView also splits there). A suspected gap for caret moves made INSIDE the gate did NOT reproduce: every gate veto settles, and settle commits the live selection (`HarnessGateCaretUndoTests`, a regression pin verified to go red when that commit is broken). | 1 | `Tests/HarnessUndoCaretTests.swift` — `HarnessUndoCaretTests` (a stock `UITextView` oracle in the same test pins the expectation) | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: undo gives a code block's LANGUAGE back wrong. Type `swift` in the language line, move the caret away and back, Backspace ×5, undo → language `s` (a second undo gives `swift`, i.e. the steps come back out of order). Typing `swift` there is also two undo steps (`swift`→`s`→none) where body typing is one. Seen 3× on K1. Legacy has no language line. Suspected: the undo baseline is not kept current for edits on the language line, which settle materialises (`settleLanguageLinePresence`). **Fixed:** the controller re-attributes the separator when a line goes empty↔non-empty, reported as a same-length replacement; `IDBlockDocumentChange.charactersChanged` now tells a re-attribution from a replacement, so it joins the open run instead of opening a step. The run-ending rule above fixes the out-of-order restore. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessCodeLanguageUndoTests.swift` — `HarnessCodeLanguageUndoTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: pasting plain text of 3+ lines into a CODE block or a PULL QUOTE ejects the last pasted line and everything after the caret into a new body paragraph: code `"Second paragraph."`, paste `"l1\nl2\nl3"` after "Second " → `[code "Second l1\nl2"] [p "l3 paragraph."]`; in a pull quote the ejected paragraph is also `italic` in the model. Two lines stay inside; quotes and paragraphs split correctly. Legacy (its own code block): all lines stay in the code. Seen 2× code, 1× pull quote on K1. **Fixed:** `declareFreshBlocks` (`+Settle.swift`) compared each fresh block with the previous — itself fresh — so only the first rejoined; a `rejoinTarget` chain now rejoins them all to the container. The code case also needed smart insert OFF in code (below): UIKit's smart-insert space is correct in a pull quote (matches UITextView and legacy) and wrong in code. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessCodeBlockPasteTests.swift` — `HarnessCodeBlockPasteTests` (code + pull-quote cases) | provisional |
| 2026-09-22 | Engine: Copy drops a BLOCK QUOTE that the selection enters from outside. `[p "First paragraph."] [quote "Second paragraph."]` → Select All → Copy puts only `"First paragraph."` on the pasteboard (plain, RTF and fragment JSON alike), so paste loses the quote and Cut discards it. Selection inside the quote copies fine; code blocks, pull quotes and plain paragraphs copy fine. Legacy control: both paragraphs. Seen 3× on K1 (6..<25, 0..<34, `selectAll:`). Suspected: `selectedGlobalRange()` / `Document.extractFragment` in `writeSelectionToPasteboard()` (`+Clipboard.swift`). **Fixed:** `Document.extractFragment`'s `.blockQuote` arm kept a quote only when its whole span incl. closing tokens was covered, which a text selection never is; a selection entering a quote now extracts its covered children as a quote. Cut fixed by the same change. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessQuoteCopyTests.swift` — `HarnessQuoteCopyTests` (plain-paragraph control inside) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: text typed INSIDE a spoiler or an inline-code (Monospace) run loses that marker when a LATER edit is undone. `First` → Format ▸ Spoiler → caret after `Fi` → type `Z` (model `[sp "FiZrst"]`, correct) → caret to the end → type `Y` → undo → `[sp "Fi"] "Z" [sp "rst"]` — the Z is revealed. Monospace the same; Bold (an engine-native trait) survives (control). Seen 2× spoiler, 1× monospace on K1. Legacy not a usable control (it never continues a spoiler into typed text at all). Suspected: the undo baseline (`commitUndoSnapshotAfterSettle`, `+UndoStack.swift`) is read from `document` before the typed character carries the markers `attributesForTypedText` copies forward (`markersThatAlwaysContinue`, `+TypedText.swift`). **Fixed:** the typed-text policy re-attributes the typed run after settle committed the baseline (and left `isEngineEditInFlight` stuck); `engineDidChangeDocument` now re-commits the baseline for such a re-attribution. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessTypedMarkerUndoTests.swift` — `HarnessTypedMarkerUndoTests` (bold case is a passing control) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: pasting the editor's own clipboard (the private fragment type — any copy/cut made in the composer) into an EMPTY paragraph leaves an extra empty paragraph after the pasted text. Cut `First paragraph.` (0..<16) + Paste → `[p "First paragraph."] [p ""] [p "Second paragraph."]` (cut/paste is not a round trip); copy a line and paste into the empty composer → two lines, the second empty. Also for a partial copy (`First`) and a bulleted-list fragment. Pasting into a non-empty paragraph and plain-text pastes are correct. Cutting a whole CODE block or PULL QUOTE (16..<35 or 0..<35) and pasting it back also ends with an extra trailing empty paragraph (not a round trip). Seen 5× (+4 container round trips) on K1. Legacy control (copy, paste into an empty last line): no extra paragraph. Suspected: `pasteFragmentFromPasteboard()` (`+Clipboard.swift`) → `Document.replacingRange` at a collapsed caret keeps the empty host paragraph after the inserted blocks. **Fixed:** `replacingRangeReportingInsertion` (Core) duplicated the empty host paragraph for an empty range in an empty paragraph (`DocumentEmptiedBlockTests`). | 1 | `Tests/HarnessFragmentPasteEmptyParagraphTests.swift` — `HarnessFragmentPasteEmptyParagraphTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: deleting or typing over a selection that starts in a paragraph and ends INSIDE (or exactly at the start of) a following BLOCK QUOTE dissolves the quote into the paragraph and leaves a stray EMPTY paragraph (in the model and in the engine text; the field stays two lines tall). `[p "First paragraph."] [quote "Second paragraph."]`, select 5..<25 → Backspace → `[p "Firstaragraph."] [p ""]`; type X → `[p "FirstXaragraph."] [p ""]`; select 5..<17 → Backspace → `[p "FirstSecond paragraph."] [p ""]`. Code blocks and pull quotes keep their tail with no stray block; Select All + type is clean for all three. Seen 4× on K1. Legacy control (its Format ▸ Quote, same selections, Backspace): `[quote "Firstaragraph."]` / `[quote "FirstSecond paragraph."]`, no stray paragraph. Suspected: the cross-block deletion rule (surface gate / `+BackspaceRules.swift`) leaves the dissolved quote's emptied child paragraph behind. **Fixed:** `settleOrphanedSecondaryLines` turned the dissolved quote's empty author line into a body paragraph; an orphaned EMPTY author line is now removed with its separator. `EngineNestedQuoteGateTests.test_aRangeEnteringANestedQuoteKeepsTheEndpointChild` pinned the old blank paragraph and was updated. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessQuoteCrossDeleteTests.swift` — `HarnessQuoteCrossDeleteTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: REDO puts the caret where it was when undo was pressed, not after the redone text. `…dog.` type "A" at 44 → move caret to 4 → undo (caret 44, correct) → redo → text `…dog.A`, selection 4..<4 (legacy/UITextView 45..<45). A caret move after the undo does not matter (move to 10, redo → still 4). No caret move between edit and undo → correct. Seen 3× on K1 (regression sweep of the undo-caret fix). Suspected: side effect of `refreshUndoBaselineSelection()` (`+UndoStack.swift`): the undo step registers its redo with the live (moved) selection. **Fixed:** the redo mirror is registered with `currentRedoSnapshot()` (`+UndoStack.swift`), whose selection is the one the change that produced the document LEFT — `EngineUndoSnapshot.editSelection`, which `refreshUndoBaselineSelection()` carries across caret moves while it moves only `selection`. The undo-caret fix keeps working (the baseline's `selection` is still live). | 1 | `Tests/HarnessRedoCaretTests.swift` — `HarnessRedoCaretTests` (stock `UITextView` oracle inside) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: deleting or typing over a selection from a paragraph INTO a following block quote leaves the caret at the END of the text, so the next keystroke lands there: select 5..<25, type X, type Q → `FirstXaragraph.Q`. Backspace 5..<25 → caret 14 (end), 5..<17 → 22 (end); legacy caret 5. Code block, pull quote and plain paragraph leave the caret at 5 (controls). Seen 4× on K1 (regression sweep of the cross-delete fix). Suspected: `settleOrphanedSecondaryLines` removes the dissolved quote's empty author line after the caret was placed, without mapping the selection. **Fixed:** that removal now maps the selection through the removed span (as `settle(_:)` does for an author line it removes); the controller had parked the caret at the end of its own edit. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessQuoteCrossDeleteCaretTests.swift` — `HarnessQuoteCrossDeleteCaretTests` (controls inside) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: Select All → Cut → Paste of a document ENDING in a container (quote, code, pull quote) appends an empty body paragraph: `[p][code]` → `[p][code][p ""]`, caret in the new paragraph. The fourth roam's fragment-paste row listed this container round trip as fixed; the paragraph case is fixed, this one is not. Seen 4× on K1. Legacy not a usable control (its quote does not survive its own cut/paste). Suspected: the empty-host rule in `replacingRangeReportingInsertion` (Core) keeps the host paragraph when the fragment's LAST block is a container. **Not a Core defect:** Core's splice drops the empty host tail; the `[p ""]` is appended by the engine's `replaceRange` caret policy (`+ArticleHost.swift`, 2026-09-17, "the caret goes after everything inserted, and the document grows a line if it must"), which adds a paragraph whenever the inserted content ends in a non-body/heading block at the document end. That policy is a product decision; left open pending it. **Closed as by design (2026-09-23):** the trailing line is the requested rule "if the inserted content ends in a block other than body/header AND the insertion is at the end of the document, a line is added" (RichTextEditor `CLAUDE.md`, 2026-09-17). The tests now pin that rule instead: blocks intact, exactly one trailing empty line, caret in it. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessContainerCutPasteRoundTripTests.swift` — `HarnessContainerCutPasteRoundTripTests` | by design |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: caret, selection highlight and handles are always system blue; the chat theme's accent is ignored. Legacy tints them with `theme.list.itemAccentColor` (white in the default dark theme, so legacy's selection is a neutral grey where the engine's is a low-contrast blue band inside a dark quote; any custom accent is likewise lost). lldb: engine `tintColor` = `systemBlueColor`, legacy = the accent. Seen 2× dark on K1 + the light-theme values. Suspected: `RichTextEditorChatInputNode.inputTintColor` / `inputCaretColor` are stored and never applied (`didChangeInputTintColor()` is empty). **Fixed:** `inputTintColor` is the node's own `tintColor` (inherited by the editor and the engine text view), `didChangeInputTintColor()` forwards `tintColorDidChange()`, and `inputCaretColor` is the editor view's tint — the legacy node's arrangement. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessCaretTintTests.swift` — `HarnessCaretTintTests` (legacy control inside; colours compared in dark traits because the day accent equals iOS 26's light `systemBlue`) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: italic on MONOSPACE text is dropped by the next edit anywhere — typing a character (same or other paragraph), Return, or Format ▸ Quote on another paragraph turns `[i,m] "st pa"` into `[m]`; bold on monospace survives, italic without monospace survives, Format ▸ Bold elsewhere does not trigger it. Legacy keeps `[i,m]`. Seen 5× on K1 (found in the format/undo stress pass). Suspected: presentation re-derivation after settle (`normalisePresentation` / bridge read-back) derives italic from the face, and the monospace face does not carry it. **Fixed:** the shared `AttributedStringMapper` rendered every inline-code run in the regular monospace face, so the italic the read-back derives from the face was gone; an italic inline-code run is now rendered in the italic monospace face (legacy canvas included). | 1 | `Tests/HarnessMonospaceItalicTests.swift` — `HarnessMonospaceItalicTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: pasting markdown text (`# Title`, `- one`, `**bold**`) lands literally; legacy parses it. The engine's `plainTextFragmentTransformer` is stored and never consulted ("RE-DEFERRED TO: R7-paste"). Reachable only since the harness wires `pastedMarkdownParser` like the app. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessMarkdownPasteTests.swift` — `HarnessMarkdownPasteTests` (red before the fix); undo shape (rich → raw → nothing), mid-paragraph, plain-prose and literal-in-code cases in `Tests/HarnessMarkdownPasteShapeTests.swift` | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: Return on an EMPTY NESTED list item ends the list instead of outdenting it one level. `1. a / (1) 1. b`, Return twice after "b" → `[ordered 0 "a"] [ordered 1 "b"] [p ""]`; mid-list (`a / b / c / d`, Return twice after nested "c") a body paragraph splits the list and "d" restarts at 1. The legacy rich editor outdents here (`DocumentCanvasView.insertParagraphBreak`, placeholder "Press return to outdent"), and the engine's own Backspace-at-start rule outdents first. Level-0 exit is correct (control). Seen 3× on K1 (lists from markdown paste, `insertText:@"\n"` via lldb). Suspected: `decideReturnInListItem` (`RichTextEngineEditorView+Lists.swift`) calls `endList` for any empty item, never `outdentItem` for `level > 0`. **Fixed:** `decideReturnInListItem` outdents an empty item with `level > 0` (`outdentItem`, keeping its marker) and ends the list only at level 0. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessNestedListReturnTests.swift` — `HarnessNestedListReturnTests` (level-0 control inside) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: list indent / outdent from the hardware keyboard (Tab / Shift-Tab) registers NO undo step; ⌘Z undoes the edit before it together with the indent (type "x" in item 1 → Shift-Tab item 3 → undo: both reverted; redo restores both). Right after a markdown paste, Tab + undo reverts the paste's rich step. `canUndo` does not change on the key. Seen 3× on K1 (key-command actions `handleListTab:` / `handleListShiftTab:` via lldb). Suspected: `indent()` / `outdent()` (`+Paragraph.swift`) go through `mutateTouchedParagraphs` → `applyEngineParallelBlocks`, which records no step (the `+Lists.swift` rules use `applyParagraphShape(_:restoring:at:)`, which does). `setParagraphStyle` / `setList` / `setAlignment` share that path (expanded-editor callers, not driven here). **Fixed:** `mutateTouchedParagraphs` (`+Paragraph.swift`) registers one step per command restoring the pre-command document and selection (inside the one `onChange` delivery; none when nothing changed), which covers `indent`/`outdent`/`setParagraphStyle`/`setList`/`setAlignment`. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessListIndentUndoTests.swift` — `HarnessListIndentUndoTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: a numbered item inside a BLOCK QUOTE continues the numbering of the list above it (`1. one / 2. two / ▌3. three`; quoting "beta" in a 4-item list draws `1. alpha / ▌2. beta / 1. gamma / 2. delta`). The legacy rich editor numbers each expanded quote/details body as its own scope (`▌1.`). Seen 2× on K1 (screenshots). Suspected: `listMarkerLabels()` (`+ListMarkers.swift`) feeds `ListNumbering` the flat roles; nothing list-less precedes a quote's first child, only its author line follows it. **Fixed:** `listMarkerLabels()` feeds `ListNumbering` a list-less boundary wherever the role's enclosing containers change, so each quote/details body numbers from 1; the ordered-marker column run breaks there too. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessQuotedListNumberingTests.swift` — `HarnessQuotedListNumberingTests` (quote-after-paragraph control inside; reads the drawn `ListMarkerLabelView`s) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: Return at the START of a heading leaves an empty heading above and demotes the heading's own text to body: `[p "intro"] [h2 "Second"]`, caret before "Second", Return → `[p "intro"] [h2 ""] [p "Second"]`. Return at the end (→ body line after) and mid-heading (tail → body, legacy's documented rule) are as designed. The legacy rich editor shares the rule (it demotes the lower half unconditionally), so this is a product-rule finding, not an engine-vs-legacy divergence. Seen 3× on K1. Suspected: the split keeps the block identity on the empty upper half, so the text lands in a fresh block that gets the bridge's default `.body`. **Fixed (product decision):** at offset 0 of a non-empty, non-list heading the gate inserts an empty BODY paragraph above and keeps the heading (`decideReturnAtStartOfHeading`, `+SurfaceGate.swift`), caret at its start; mid/end rules unchanged. Recorded in the RichTextEditor `CLAUDE.md` beside the split rule. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessHeadingReturnAtStartTests.swift` — `HarnessHeadingReturnAtStartTests` (end-of-heading control inside) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: pasting a long rich document is superlinear and freezes the composer. K1 debug, CPU s per markdown paste of N formatted paragraphs: 25 → 0.23, 50 → 0.59, 100 → 2.3, 200 → 12.9, 250 → 23.8; the 1000-line mixed document (200 headings, 200 formatted paragraphs, 400 list items): 149 s frozen, process +1.25 GB. The same document pasted as the editor's own fragment costs the same (so not the parser); 500 unformatted list items cost 1.3 s. `sample` of the main thread: ~90 % under `id_reconcileDerivedStateForDocumentChange:` → `IDBlockStackCanvasView updateForDocumentChange:width:`, ~60 % in the per-block `id_emptyLine{Attributes,CaretOffset}ForIdentifier:` → `installEmptyLineProviders()` closures → `role(forEngineIdentity:)`, whose `isApplyingHostDocument` fallback does `engine.tgid_blockIdentifiers.firstIndex(of:)` (array rebuilt + bridged per call ⇒ O(n²)). Debug-build numbers; the growth rate is the finding. **Fixed:** three O(n²) paths, each measured with `sample` on the hosted test: `role(forEngineIdentity:)` now uses `tgid_index(ofBlockIdentifier:)` (map cached per `IDBlockDocument.blocks` array); `IDBlockStackCanvasView updateForDocumentChange:width:` counts its subviews instead of calling `-subviews` per block; `IDCanonicalTextProjection` caches its string per `blocks` array and `IDCanonicalTextStorageAdapter` applies `NSTextStorage`'s attribute fixing to one working copy adopted once (`id_adoptCanonicalAttributes:changedRange:`). Hosted test runner: 50 → 0.51 s / 200 → 12.9 s before, 0.11 s / 0.37 s after (3.4-3.7×). | 1 | `Tests/HarnessLargeRichPasteScalingTests.swift` — `HarnessLargeRichPasteScalingTests` (asserts 4× paragraphs < 8× time; K1 today ~22×) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Engine: copying a TABLE writes plain text without its cells — Select All over `para before / table / para after` → `"para before\n\npara after"`; a selection inside the table → `""`. In-app paste is unaffected (fragment; legacy composer latches and restores the table too); any plain-text destination loses the table. Seen 2× on K1. Suspected: `RichTextEditorClipboard.pasteboardItem(for:)` → `externalChecklistPlainText` → `blockPlainText`'s `default: ""` for `.table` (Core already has `tableFlattenedText`). Shared with the legacy rich editor's copy. **Fixed:** `blockPlainText` (Core) returns a table as its `tableFlattenedText` rows, one line per row; the fragment rep is unchanged. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessTableCopyPlainTextTests.swift` — `HarnessTableCopyPlainTextTests` | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Legacy: a Format command cannot be toggled OFF over a selection that carries it throughout but carries another format on part of it — it re-applies. `quick brown` Bold, `brown` Italic, `quick brown` Bold → still `[b] "quick " [b,i] "brown"` (engine: bold removed, italic kept). Same for Spoiler over a partly bold range. Seen 2× on K1 (Bold, Spoiler). Suspected: `chatTextInputAddFormattingAttribute` (`ChatPresentationInterfaceState/Sources/ChatTextFormat.swift`) removes only when one enumerated run EQUALS the selection; `enumerateAttributes` splits runs at every attribute change. Shared with upstream. **Fixed:** the toggle now asks whether THIS attribute covers the whole selection (`enumerateAttribute(attribute, …)`, no gap ⇒ remove, else add). Callers: the chat panel, `AttachmentTextInputPanelNode` and `ChatControllerKeyShortcuts` — all toggles, all want this. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessLegacyFormatToggleOffTests.swift` — `HarnessLegacyFormatToggleOffTests` (uniform-selection control inside) | provisional |
| 2026-09-23 | Legacy: every Format-menu command empties the undo stack — the command cannot be undone, nor anything typed before it. Type `X` (canUndo true) → Bold/Italic on `quick` → canUndo false, undo/redo do nothing (engine: undo reverts the format, then the typing). Seen 2× on K1 (Bold, Italic). Suspected: the command's state is pushed back by `updateInputTextState`, which assigns the `UITextView`'s `attributedText`, and a programmatic set clears its undo stack. **Fixed:** `ChatInputTextView.performUndoableTextChange(_:)` wraps the panel's Format commands: inside it `attributedText` writes go through the text storage (attribute-only when the string is unchanged), and one undo step restoring the pre-command text + selection is registered (its restore registers the redo). The loupe DID move the caret (43) when it began, and UIKit put it back on release. A loupe that begins within 40pt of the caret (`UITextRefinementTouchBehavior`, `TextRefinement_NearCaretDistance`) is treated as dragging the caret by its offset, so on release it re-places the caret at the start caret plus the translation (here: the old caret) — unless the assistant's `autoscrolled` bit is set. The stock text view sets that bit on the first tick of its autoscroll session, which in a field shorter than two 32pt bands (a composer of up to about three lines) starts for EVERY point and cancels after that tick, so legacy kept the caret at the finger. The engine's `startAutoscroll:` (`IDBlockTextView+PrivateInteractionCallbacks.m`) declined with `autoscrollWillNotStart` (nothing for it to scroll), and its own `IDVerticalAutoscroll` ticks never set the bit either. Fix: the engine reports a tick (`-[IDUIKitInteractionCapability autoscrollDidTick]`, guarded `setAutoscrolled:`) wherever the stock session would have started (`IDVerticalAutoscrollStockSessionWouldStart`, the stock rule from its disassembly) and on every tick of its own session. Traced on K1 with lldb, engine vs legacy in one process (branch at `+1248` of the touch behavior's state-3 path: `autoscrolled` 1 on legacy, 0 on the engine). Decision: a new host hook `tgid_markedTextFilter` (both marked-text doors and a commit's `insertText:` while marked) lets the editor flatten a candidate holding a line break with `oneLineText` when the selection is in a one-line field; the composition stays open, the in-marked selection is clamped. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessCompositionNewlineInOneLineFieldTests.swift` — `HarnessCompositionNewlineInOneLineFieldTests` (4 green) | provisional |
| 2026-10-02 | Engine (medium; p24, found by the new `chatMention` fuzz op, edited seeds 69 and 80, reduced): a code block's LANGUAGE line holding a space at either END — typed (`"MX "`, the space before the next word of "objective c") or the trailing space the avatar menu's Mention leaves (`"MXHarness "`) — is kept by the engine and TRIMMED by the read-back, so the engine and the model disagree. The next re-render from the model (a theme change, auto-night) drops the space and the selection JUMPS to the end of the code body, wherever it was: `[p "top"] [code lang="MX" "let x = 1"] [p ""]`, caret after `MX`, type a space → caret 7; dark theme and back → line `MX`, caret 16; with the caret moved to 1 first → 16. Model offsets are a character off from then on: seed 80 selected a whole line by its model offsets after such a language and Backspace left an empty paragraph (the `line` op's "deleting a whole line changed the line after it"). Controls: an inner space (`"MX c"`) and no space keep the caret. The fuzz's projection compares languages trimmed, so its axis check never saw it. Suspected: the language read-back trims (`block(for:from:)`) while the engine keeps what was typed; the re-render restores the selection on a stale offset. Keep the language untrimmed while editing (trim on send / for an emptied line), or re-render without moving the selection. **Fixed (p24 fix wave):** root cause: the read-back trimmed the language while the engine kept the line. Decision: the MODEL keeps the language line as typed (only an empty line → nil; `EngineBlockRole.merging`, `languagesAsTyped` removed, `replayDocument` is the model), and the wire trims it — `chatInputCodeLanguageForSending` (TelegramCore) at the `Pre` entity (`generateChatInputTextEntities`) and the rich `preformatted` block. Trimming at settle was rejected: it eats the space between words typed one keystroke at a time ("objective c"). The fuzz projection compares languages exactly now; its per-step language-edge check is gone. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessCodeLanguageEdgeWhitespaceTests.swift` — `HarnessCodeLanguageEdgeWhitespaceTests` (5 green) | provisional |
| 2026-10-02 | Engine (defence in depth, p24 fix wave; the p23 audit's observation (b), unreachable since p23): when the engine held more blocks than there were roles (a rendered block carrying `U+000A`), the layout's recovery "by identity" (`recoverRolesFromEngineIdentities`) rebuilt from a table the document setter had recorded POSITIONALLY after the engine's split, so roles shifted: `[code lang="x\ny" "body"]` read back `[code lang="x" "y"] [p "body"]`, cells moved next to a table, an authored quote's last child became its author. **Fixed:** the setter records split-aware (`recordBlockIdentitiesAfterReplacingSpan`: each rendered block's role goes to the FIRST engine block of its lines, the lines after it stay unknown, the blocks after the span are matched from the end), and the recovery makes an unknown block a body paragraph at its predecessor's depth (out of a block quote after its author). The mismatch is forced through test hooks (`EngineDocumentBridge.testHookRenderedBlocks`, `testHookExpectsRoleCountMismatch`), no product edit; neuter-checked: with the positional record back, all four split cases go red, the control stays green. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessRoleRecoveryByIdentityTests.swift` — `HarnessRoleRecoveryByIdentityTests` (4 cases + 1 control, green) | provisional |
| 2026-10-12 | Legacy (code review of the InputDec squash): the chat's ⌘B / ⌘I / ⌘U / ⇧⌘M / ⇧⌘X / ⇧⌘P / ⇧⌘N recorded no undo step on the legacy composer — undo after it took back the typing before it and left the format. The shortcut's legacy path went through the chat's `updateTextInputStateAndMode`, whose echo is an attribute-only write. **Fixed:** `ChatTextInputPanelNode.performLegacyFormatShortcut` wraps the transform in the text view's `performUndoableTextChange`, as the Format menu does. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessLegacyFormatShortcutUndoTests.swift` (red before the fix) | provisional |
| 2026-10-12 | Legacy (review of the fix wave): the first fix for the row above wrapped EVERY chat-side sync in an undo step on the legacy node (`performUndoableInputChange`), so syncs that changed nothing left undo steps that did nothing. **Fixed:** the legacy override is gone (back to the protocol default); only the format shortcuts take a step. Neuter-checked: reinstating the override fails the test. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessLegacyPhantomUndoStepTests.swift` | provisional |
| 2026-10-12 | Legacy (code review of the InputDec squash): the legacy text view's `attributedText` setter wrote in place when the new value had the "same characters", decided with Swift `String ==` (canonical equivalence), so a set in the other Unicode normalization form walked the storage with the wrong length: `NSRangeException` for a longer value, stale code units for a shorter one. **Fixed:** compared as code units (`NSString isEqual:`). | 1 | `Tests/HarnessLegacyNormalizationSetTests.swift` (red before the fix; the longer case trapped) | provisional |
| 2026-10-12 | Legacy (code review of the InputDec squash): a custom emoji inserted from the emoji keyboard after inline code inherited the code (`chatTextInputStateInsertingText`), so the message went out with overlapping `Code` and `CustomEmoji`. **Fixed:** inline code is not inherited onto a custom-emoji range; plain text still continues it. The review's sibling prediction for a CODE BLOCK did not hold (the state's `ChatInputContent` holds a code block as plain runs, so the emoji arrives as its alt text) and is pinned instead. | 1 | `Tests/HarnessEmojiInsertAfterInlineCodeTests.swift` (inline case red before the fix; code-block case a pin) | provisional |

## Harness fixes (not product defects)

2026-09-22, from the same roam:

- **Emoji crashed the app**: `SharedAccountContext.accountManager` was unprovided and traps by design; it now returns the manager the harness account was opened through (`HarnessAccountContextTests.testTheAccountManagerIsTheAccountsOwn`).
- **`-harness-theme dark` did nothing**: it is now applied to the presentation data and the root's interface style (`testTheDarkThemeReachesThePresentationData`). The default dark theme's accent is white, and the send icon is drawn white for a `.night` base theme, so the send arrow is invisible in dark mode here. That is the theme choice, not a missing asset.
- **The readout went stale** on selection-only and formatting-only changes: it now also refreshes when the panel pushes an input state (`HarnessPanelHost.inputStateDidChange`).
- **Panel icons rendered blank**: the app now bundles `//submodules/TelegramUI:TelegramUIAssets`.
- **Tier 2 runs on iOS 27.0** (`HarnessUITestRunner`, iPhone 18 Pro), matching K1; the focusing-tap defect had been seen only there.

2026-09-24, twelfth wave:

- **The fuzz's Send did not always clear.** It cleared only when the model had flat text or more than one block, so a single container with no flat text (`[quote [pull ""] author=" "]`, seed 30) was never cleared and the "typing after Send is plain" check reported the `z` typed into the uncleared pull quote. The clear is now always issued.
- **Two timing tests took single samples** (`HarnessNestedQuoteKeystrokeScalingTests` median-of-5, `HarnessLargeRichPasteScalingTests` one sample per size). Both now compare the MINIMUM per size after a warm-up: scheduler noise only adds time. Thresholds unchanged.

## Harness gaps (open, not product defects)

2026-09-22, second roam:

- ~~**Legacy Format menu does nothing.**~~ **Fixed 2026-09-22:** `HarnessPanelHost` now pushes a changed `effectiveInputState` back into the panel, as `ChatControllerNode` does (`HarnessChatSurfaceTests.testTheLegacyBoldCommandFormatsTheSelection`).
- ~~**`openLinkEditing` is an empty closure.**~~ **Fixed 2026-09-22:** Format ▸ Link opens a harness alert (`harness.linkField`, Apply / Remove / Cancel); the answer is applied by the chat's own code on each backend (`testFormatLinkAppliesOnTheEngine` / `…OnLegacy`). Hosted tests answer through `HarnessRootViewController.linkPromptOverride`. Format ▸ Date is still a no-op on the engine (a product TODO).
- ~~**No placeholder and a blank voice/mic button.**~~ **Fixed 2026-09-22 (placeholder pinned by `testTheEmptyComposerShowsTheMessagePlaceholder`; mic icon confirmed rendering on K1):** the chat now has a rendered peer (`HarnessPanelHost.chatPeer`, a 1:1 chat instead of Saved Messages) and the app bundles `TelegramUIResources` and the LegacyComponents resources/assets.
- **K1 boots into hardware-keyboard mode** for every fresh app process (`[[UIKeyboardImpl activeInstance] isInHardwareKeyboardMode] == YES`), so no software keyboard appears; `GSEventSetHardwareKeyboardAttached` did not clear it this time. What worked, per launch: focus, then lldb `[[UIKeyboardImpl activeInstance] setInHardwareKeyboardMode:NO]` and resign/become first responder. HID key events (`axe key`, `key-sequence`, `key-combo`, XcodeBuildMCP `key_press`) did not reach either backend at all, even with a fresh `hid-broker`, so ⌘-shortcuts, arrows and ⌘-Return could not be exercised. `axe describe-ui` also times out for a few seconds after an `axe batch`.

2026-09-22, third roam:

- **Mention / hashtag / command / emoji-keyword suggestions cannot appear.** `ChatTextInputPanelNode` does not compute input queries; the chat does (`inputContextQueriesForChatPresentationIntefaceState` + `contextQueryResultStateForChatInterfacePresentationState` in `TelegramUI/Sources/ChatInterface{InputContexts,StateContextQueries}.swift`) and shows the results in `ChatControllerNode`'s context panel. Reproducing that means porting both functions onto `HarnessPanelHost`'s `effectiveInputState` and hosting a results panel: beyond the composer panel's scope. What IS in scope and untested: the engine inserting a picked mention (`chatTextInputAddMentionAttribute` pushed back through `updateInputTextState`).
- **Send-button long press does nothing**: `displaySendMessageOptions` is not provided in the harness's `ChatPanelInterfaceInteraction`, so the send-options preview is unreachable.
- **Backend switch reseeds from the scenario**: the rebuilt panel starts from the scenario seed, not from what was on screen (no crash, no corruption; harness behaviour).
- **Driving notes (K1).** The Format submenu is taller than the space above the keyboard; its lower items (Strikethrough / Underline / Code) are reached by a slow `axe swipe --duration 1.5 --delta 5` inside the menu. `axe tap`/`touch` did not land on the harness link alert's buttons, XcodeBuildMCP `tap` on the snapshot ref did. The software-keyboard mode survived app relaunches this session once set. lldb (`insertText:`, `deleteBackward`, `setSelectedTextRange:`, `copy:`/`paste:`, `undoManager undo`) on the `TGIDObservingBlockTextView` found by class is the fastest reliable driver; Swift expressions do not evaluate in this binary.


2026-09-23, fourth roam:

- **Markdown-on-paste is not wired.** The chat sets `ChatTextInputPanelNode.pastedMarkdownParser` to `chatInputContentFromPastedMarkdown` (`ChatControllerNode.swift:1017`, `ChatInterfaceStateInputPanels.swift:515`); `HarnessPanelHost` does not, so a plain-text paste of `- a`, `# h`, a table or ``` fences stays literal on the engine and never latches on legacy. The parser lives in `TelegramUI`, which the harness does not link. Until then lists / headings / checklists can only reach the composer as an editor FRAGMENT: write the `org.telegram.richtexteditor.fragment` JSON (a `Document`, `schemaVersion` 1) to the pasteboard and send `paste:` — that is how this roam exercised them.
- **No Text Size knob.** `presentationData.chatFontSize` is the default; a `-harness-font-size <PresentationFontSize raw value>` launch argument applied where the presentation data is built (the same place as `-harness-theme`) would let Text Size be exercised. Not implemented.
- **Send is synthetic.** `simulateSend` records the panel's content when `plainText` is non-empty; the chat's own trimming (whitespace-only messages, trailing empty paragraphs) is in `ChatController`, so those send edge cases cannot be judged here. The panel's own Send/mic switch is real and matches legacy for whitespace-only input and after undo-to-empty.
- **Driving notes (K1).** The engine's Format menu can be driven without UI: read `tgid_editMenuElementsProvider` off the `TGIDObservingBlockTextView` in lldb, call it with `@[]` (needs a non-empty selection), find the `Format` submenu child by title and `performWithSender:target:`. Legacy's format commands are `@objc` on the panel (`formatAttributesQuote:`, `formatAttributesSpoiler:` …; find the panel by walking `$tv`'s superviews for an `asyncdisplaykit_node` whose class name contains `ChatTextInputPanelNode`). `UIKit` does not `@import` into lldb here, so every message to an `id` needs a cast (`(NSString *)[e title]`, `object_getClass` instead of `-class`). `pgrep` must be scoped to K1's device path: other agents' harness processes on other sims match `ComposerHarness.app` too.

2026-09-23, fifth roam:

- **Text Size cannot be exercised on this branch.** `-harness-font-size` reaches the presentation data, but this branch's `ChatTextInputPanelNode` still carries upstream's `if "".isEmpty { baseFontSize = 17.0 }` pins, so the engine renders at 17 pt at every size and legacy starts at 17 pt and jumps to the Text Size on the first keystroke (typing attributes are unpinned). Master replaced the pins with `ChatTextInputFontMetrics.swift` (`chatTextInputBaseFontSize`) and feeds it to the engine too; the branch does not contain master. Re-run the Text Size checks after merging master.
- **`xcrun simctl pbcopy` does not reach K1's pasteboard** (`pbinfo`: "Pasteboard is empty"; in-app `pasteboardTypes` empty), so the paste-prompt repro (another app's content on the clipboard) could not be re-run on screen. In-app `[[UIPasteboard generalPasteboard] setString:]` via lldb works for everything else, but that content is the app's own and never prompts.
- **The send button does not respond to `axe touch` or `axe tap` on either backend** (one-paragraph, unfocused and focused), so repeated sending could not be stressed on screen; hosted tests reach it through `simulateSend`.
- **The focusing-tap regression check is environment-sensitive.** With the software keyboard on (lldb `GSEventSetHardwareKeyboardAttached(0, 0)` then `setInHardwareKeyboardMode:NO` and resign/become), a fresh-launch `axe touch --delay 0.08/0.1` just right of the seeded caret raised Select / Select All on the engine 16/20 and on LEGACY 7/20; at the Tier-2 test's own point (dx 0.6, one-paragraph) 4/5 engine and 4/5 legacy. In hardware-keyboard mode legacy raised it 3/4. The fourth roam measured legacy 0/5, so either this session's input timing differs or iOS 27.0 now raises the caret menu on a focusing tap for stock text views too; the engine rate stays above legacy's. Not filed; the Tier-2 test's green does not establish 0 on screen.
- **Driving notes (K1).** A reusable lldb Python helper (`hfind`/`hsel`/`hfmt <Format item>`/`hdump`/`hcur`/`hpanel`/`hspin`) made each repro one batch attach. Edits issued in ONE lldb expression batch without a run-loop turn between them land in one undo group (UIKit groups by event), which reads as "undo reverts five formats at once" — spin the main run loop (`[[NSRunLoop mainRunLoop] runUntilDate:]`) between steps. The readout's `selection` for a caret in a code block's LANGUAGE line is the model position (end of the block), not the UITextInput offset; read `selectedTextRange` for the latter.

2026-09-23, sixth roam:

- **Emoji suggestions cannot appear, for two harness reasons.** The panel's suggestion popover (`updateInputField`, `ChatTextInputPanelNode.swift:~4112`) is not keyword-driven: it fires when the character before the caret is a single emoji and asks `EmojiSuggestionsComponent.suggestionData` for animated-emoji ALTERNATIVES from the account's `CloudEmojiPacks` + featured packs, which the offline harness account has none of (so the result is empty and no view is shown). Even with data it could not render: it also requires `self.presentationContext` (the harness builds the panel with `presentationContext: nil`, like the `ChatInterfaceStateInputPanels.swift:484` fallback; the chat's main panel at `ChatControllerNode.swift:939` passes a real `ChatPresentationContext`) and `viewForOverlayContent`, which `ChatControllerNode` adds to its overlay node and the harness never adds anywhere. The same `presentationContext` gates `emojiViewProvider`, so inline CUSTOM emoji cannot draw in the harness either. `:smile`-style keyword completion is not a composer-panel feature at all (the chat's input-context queries; see the third roam). Typing `hi 😀` on K1: nothing appears.
- **The readout and the synthetic send treat a table-only composer as empty.** `ComposerModelDump` prints `hasText=` from `content.plainText`, which excludes table cells, so a composer holding only a table reads `hasText=false` while the panel correctly shows Send; `HarnessRootViewController.simulateSend` guards on the same `plainText.isEmpty`, so a table-only message cannot be "sent" here. The readout also prints `table rows=N` without cell text, so cell edits are only visible through `textInRange:` on the engine view.
- **Markdown parser limits seen (the app's parser, not the composer):** `#### H4` and deeper arrive as `heading3` (the model has three levels); `||spoiler||` stays literal; an ordered list's start number is dropped (`5. five` → `1.`). Not filed.
- **Driving notes (K1).** A 20 s lldb expression timeout silently cut a long `paste:` short, and a single-thread (`SetTryAllThreads(false)`) expression around `paste:` deadlocked; time long operations PASSIVELY instead: schedule with `performSelector:withObject:afterDelay:`, detach, and read the process's CPU time with `ps -o time=` until it stops moving (polling with repeated lldb attaches pauses the process and inflated the times 2-5×). `/usr/bin/sample <pid>` works on a simulator process and gives the hot path. `ListMarkerLabelView`s are subviews of the engine text view; their `label` is readable with `Mirror`.


## Closed: focusing tap on K1 (2026-09-23)

The fifth roam saw the edit menu on a fresh focusing tap at 16/20 (engine) and 7/20 (legacy). Most of
that was the input driver: a long-running `axe hid-broker` delivers stale or doubled touches, and a
doubled tap IS a tap on the caret. With the broker restarted every five launches the controller
measured engine 7/20 vs legacy 3/20 at (262, 820); with a fresh broker once, 3/10 vs 0/10. Legacy is
not at zero, so the residual engine excess is inside the driver's noise. The Tier-2 reproduction
(`testTheFocusingTapDoesNotRaiseTheEditMenu`, clean XCUITest input) passes 0/5 on the current build.
**Closed 2026-09-23:** re-checked with clean XCUITest input, 20 fresh launches on the iOS 27.0
runner: 0/20. The K1 counts were the driver. Driving note: kill `axe hid-broker` before any counted
run, and never count a timing-sensitive defect through `axe` alone.

2026-09-23, seventh roam (legacy as the subject):

- **Legacy RTL alignment (observed, not filed).** An Arabic or Hebrew paragraph in the legacy field is drawn LEFT-aligned, although its paragraph style is `Alignment Natural, BaseWritingDirection Natural` — a stock `UITextView` with the same text in the same process right-aligns it, and so does the engine. Likely the legacy text container's custom line-fragment code (`ChatInputTextNode.swift:322`); needs a product call on whether legacy should follow the paragraph direction.
- **Legacy: a paste on an empty last line adds a blank line above the pasted text** (`…\n` + paste `Hello` → `…\n\nHello`). Not a defect: a stock `UITextView` does the same (iOS smart insert), measured in the same process.
- **List marker column (observed, not filed).** A one-item ordered run draws `1. alpha` with a gap; a multi-item run draws `1.alpha` tight, right-aligned in a column. Seen beside a quoted list item. Cosmetic.
- **Table plain text uses a space between cells** (`a b` / `1 2`), which is ambiguous for multi-word cells; a tab would round-trip into spreadsheets. Not filed.
- **Driving notes (K1).** The harness link alert can be answered from lldb: take the root's `presentedViewController`, set `textFields[0].text`, and `_dismissAnimated:triggeringAction:triggeredByPopoverDimmingView:dismissCompletion:` with the Apply action. Legacy's Format commands are `formatAttributes<Name>:` on the panel (Bold, Italic, Monospace, Strikethrough, Underline, Quote, CodeBlock, Spoiler, Link). `insertText:` with non-ASCII text must be built with `\u`/`\U` escapes inside `@"…"`; a Python `unicode_escape` round trip mangles it into Latin-1. `NSRange` is not a known type in lldb here; use `setSelectedTextRange:` or `(struct _NSRange){a,b}`. The list Tab key-command actions (`handleListTab:` / `handleListShiftTab:`) live on the `RichTextEngineEditorView`, not the text view (sending them to the text view raises).

2026-09-23, eighth roam (sweep of the seventh wave + keyboard-driven input):

- **Legacy RTL alignment — verdict: long-standing, a side effect of TextKit 1, not a regression.** The legacy field is TextKit 1 (`ChatInputLegacyLayoutManager`, `textLayoutManager == nil`). In the same process a STOCK `UITextView(usingTextLayoutManager: false)` with the legacy view's own attributed text puts the Arabic line at x = 5 (left), and a TextKit 2 `UITextView` at x = 209.7 (right); `.natural` alignment resolves by content only under TextKit 2. Legacy has been TextKit 1 since `b4dd3591af` (2024-05-23, "Blockquote experiments": `useModernImpl = !"".isEmpty`, i.e. the TextKit 2 `ChatInputTextNewInternal` gated off); `cfe347f30d` (2026-06-09, "Cleanup") only deleted the dead branch. Nothing in the alignment/paragraph-style code (`textAttributedStringForStateText` passes `writingDirection: nil`) changed. Right-aligning RTL on legacy would need an explicit per-paragraph `baseWritingDirection`/alignment; a product call.
- **Legacy link Remove is asynchronous**, not broken: the harness alert's Remove lands after the alert's dismissal (~1 s); reading the model right after the answer shows the link still there. Wait before reading.
- **Engine model keeps adjacent runs with identical printed attributes unmerged** (`"😀" | "漢" | "ع"`, `"abc te" | "h" | " xyz"` all `[]` or all the same link). Either an attribute the readout does not print differs (the fallback face?) or runs are not coalesced; content equality (`ChatTextInputState ==`) then differs for identical text. Not filed.
- **Legacy `contentOffset.y` is 3.67 with the caret at 0** in a 30-line message (the first line's top is clipped by 3.67 pt). Inset finding, provisional by rule; not filed.
- **⌘-shortcuts were not reached**: HID key events still do not reach either backend (second roam's gap).
- **Driving notes (K1).** The software keyboard is drivable with `axe touch --down --up --delay 0.05` on the key rects (points = screenshot px / 3; with the 402×874 layout: row 1 y = 618, row 2 y = 672, row 3 y = 726, space (201, 780), suggestion slots y = 571 at x = 67 / 201 / 334); autocorrect, predictive acceptance and the double-space period all fire. `lldb --batch`: `br delete` asks for confirmation and eats the NEXT script line (use `br delete -f`); breakpoints are ignored inside `expr`, so fire UI actions from an `NSTimer scheduledTimerWithTimeInterval:repeats:block:` and `process continue`; `dispatch_get_main_queue` is not a symbol here. `expr -l objc -- @import UIKit` works and makes `CGRect`/`UITextView *` usable.

2026-09-23, ninth roam (sweep of the eighth wave on K1 + the seeded fuzz):

- **Sweep of the eighth wave's fixes — all hold on K1.** Engine: a copied word pasted mid-quote, mid-code and into an empty quote line stays inside (a PULL QUOTE still splits into `[pull "Second "] [p "First"] [pull "paragraph."]`, one undo restores it — the documented limit); autocorrect (`teh`→`the`) and predictive acceptance (`hel`→`held`) keep spoiler, monospace and bold on the real keyboard; `is ` and `!` typed inside a link keep one link, `.` at its end stays outside. Legacy: pasting the composer's own copy undoes in one step and keeps the Bold and typing steps before it (redo chain intact); Backspace at a document-start quote unquotes (one undo re-quotes); Quote and Code stay undoable after typing and two caret moves, redo restores. In-place echo side effects looked for and not found: Send with trailing bold clears to plain typing attributes and an empty undo stack; clearing a quoted+bold message by Select All + Backspace leaves plain typing, two undos restore it, caret moves survive echoes.
- **The fuzz** (`Tests/HarnessComposerFuzzTests.swift`): 20 seeds × 40 ops per backend, ~57 s engine, ~52 s legacy undo mix, ~35 s legacy with Quote/Code. Engine swept 1-80, legacy 1-70. Model/engine comparison is line-wise over the documented projection: language line first (trimmed on read-back), author lines only when the bridge emits them (an empty pull quote has none), `"\n"`↔`U+2028` inside every block. A paste's change notification reaches the chat's state later than a keystroke's (0.1 s, not 0.03 s), so each invariant is re-read once after 0.3 s before it counts.
- **Engine verifiers trap in the test process.** `verifyPresentationIsDerived` / `verifyDerivedViewsAreComplete` `assertionFailure` whenever `XCTestCase` exists, killing the whole bundle run on the first drift. The fuzz and the new drift tests switch it off (`FuzzEngineVerifier`, `@testable import RichTextEngineEditor`, now a dep of `ComposerHarnessTestsLib`) and ask `presentationDrift(atBlock:)` directly.
- **Bazel limits for sweeps.** A 60-seed sweep exceeds the default 300 s test timeout (`--test_timeout=900`), and output past 1 MB is dropped from stdout: read `bazel-testlogs/Tests/ComposerHarness/ComposerHarnessTests/test.log`. `--test_env=FUZZ_SEEDS=a-b` / `FUZZ_VERBOSE=1` reach the test process.
- **An ObjC exception in a hosted test has no stack in the log.** `objc_setExceptionPreprocessor` (via `dlsym`) printing `Thread.callStackSymbols`, plus an `NSTextStorage.didProcessEditingNotification` observer printing a stack per edit, gave both halves of the legacy crash's mechanism in one run.
- **Driving notes (K1).** The engine's Format ▸ Link needs ~1.5 s after `hfmt Link` before the alert can be answered, or the link silently does not apply. The double-space period did not fire with the 0.12 s tap spacing of `kbd.sh`. Tracing a keyboard action: run `xcrun lldb -p <pid> --batch` in the background with auto-continuing breakpoints and an in-app `NSTimer` that sends SIGINT, type with `kbd.sh` meanwhile.

2026-09-23, tenth wave (fixes for the ninth roam, by the controller's subagent):

- **The fuzz holds no exclusions and is green**: 30 seeds × 60 ops, 0 failing (177.5 s). Every `FuzzKnownDefect` signature was fixed and retired; the enum is kept empty for the next filed one.
- **A wedged keyboard task queue hangs every hosted test at its first selection change.** Seen once on the reused `BAZEL_TEST_iPhone 17_26.5` after a harness app launched outside the runner was left stuck: `sample` on the test process showed the main thread in `-[_UIKeyboardStateManager selectionWillChange:]` → `-[UIKeyboardTaskQueue waitUntil…]` (a condition-lock wait) for the whole run, so the test timed out with no failure named (and `print` output, being buffered, was lost — use `NSLog` in a probe). `simctl shutdown` + `boot` of that simulator cleared it. Never launch the harness app on a Bazel test simulator by hand; an `lldb --batch` attach to it left the process a zombie that also blocked the runner's pre-action uninstall.
- **Driving note:** the fuzz's ops are not re-snapped on replay, so a greedy reducer that drops ops can place a selection mid-surrogate and wander into a different defect (seed 18's first reduction ended in a `U+FFFD` split that is a reducer artifact); key the reducer on the specific model signature, not on the failure kind.

2026-09-23, eleventh wave (wide fuzz sweep, extended op mix, K1 sweep of the tenth wave):

- **Sweeps.** The old op mix ran seeds 31-207 (31-85 298 s, 86-125 266 s, 126-165 293 s, 166-205 544 s with stalls). Two new failures came out of it: 35 and 82. The mix was then extended (see the fuzz header), which changes every seed's stream, so the old mix's 208-300 no longer exist as such. The extended mix ran seeds 1-300: nine new defects, all red and all recognised by `FuzzKnownDefect`. The default 30 seeds are green in ~225 s.
- **Stalls on the reused `BAZEL_TEST_iPhone 17_26.5` are environmental, and the runner can see them.** Several single ops took 18-414 s, and two runs hit the 1200 s timeout. A `sample` of the stuck test process showed a main thread blocked on a system service every time:
  - `_UIKeyboardStateManager` → `UIKeyboardTaskQueue lockWhenReadyForMainThread`, with the log line "TIKeyboardInputManagerClient … will retry sending syncToKeyboardState" and, once, "closed connection to keyboard daemon … check for kbd crash logs";
  - `UITextChecker isWordInUserDictionaries` (spell check) on a dispatch semaphore;
  - `-[_UIConcretePasteboard string]` → `_coerceItemToClass`: the paste prompt, meaning foreign clipboard content arrived mid-run.

  Replays of the same seeds ran in seconds. **Driving note:** reboot that simulator BETWEEN Bazel runs when they start (`xcrun simctl shutdown <udid> && xcrun simctl boot <udid>`; the runner reuses it and its pre-action reinstalls the harness), never during one. The fuzz now re-writes its own pasteboard content before any paste when `UIPasteboard.general.changeCount` moved since its last write (reading `changeCount` reads no content), so the prompt cannot recur from the fuzz.
- **A failed test run stalls in `simctl diagnose`.** After any failing test, xcodebuild runs `simctl diagnose … --timeout=600` on the test simulator (despite `-collect-test-diagnostics=never`), and the run sits there for up to 10 min after the tests end. Killing that process (it only collects logs) returns the run at once. The scratch runner that drove this wave did so automatically.
- **`FUZZ SLOW`.** Every op or undo/redo step over 1 s is printed. That is how seed 51's nested-quote keystroke cost was found (`HarnessNestedQuoteKeystrokeScalingTests`).
- **Harness gaps.**
  - A table arranged WITHOUT column specs (`ChatInputTable(rows:)`, as a draft persisted before `columns` existed would decode: `decodeIfPresent … ?? []`) trips `assertionFailure("table … has a role but no grid — it will not be drawn")` in `syncTableChrome` and draws nothing. Drafts are out of scope, so this is not filed; real producers (paste, InstantPage conversion) always supply columns.
  - `formatThrice` checks inline formats only, because a block format moves the engine axis (a code block's language line), so fixed offsets select different text on the third pass.
  - The details and table fuzz starting documents arrive by `setInputContent`: the composer cannot create details, and tables arrive by pipe-table paste.
- **K1 sweep of the tenth wave's fixes (all lldb-driven, readout plus screenshots).** Hold on screen:
  - pull-quote Italic (the slant is kept; toggling is a no-op in model and on screen) and Monospace (upright pill);
  - the inline-code pill is continuous over typed `ZZ` and an emoji;
  - the language line stays unformatted under Spoiler / Italic / Strikethrough;
  - Return over a cross-block selection gives two paragraphs with the caret at line 2's start;
  - a copied word pasted twice inside a pull quote stays inside, caret drawn after it;
  - the link underline is drawn.

  The paste caret after a clear has a gap: Send → typing → paste leaves a stale caret (`HarnessPasteCaretAfterSendTests`). A spoiler is revealed in the block holding the caret and dusted elsewhere, which is by design. Nothing else looked off.

2026-09-24, p11 roam (IME, key commands, emoji, endurance, accessibility):

- **New fuzz ops** (`HarnessComposerFuzzTests`): `compose(FuzzIME, FuzzCommit, selecting:)` — Japanese, kana, Korean and pinyin (with a selected sub-range) candidate sequences through `setMarkedText(_:selectedRange:)`, committed by `unmarkText`, `insertText` of the final candidate, or a cancel; `keyMove(action, extend:)` — the twelve private arrow-key actions UIKit performs for ←/→/↑/↓ with ⌥/⌘/⇧; and emoji-keyboard clusters in the insertions (flag, skin tone, ZWJ profession, keycap, VS16/VS15, ZWJ flag). The mix is now `random.int(135)`, so every seed's stream changed. Invariants: nothing left marked after a commit; a cancelled composition at a caret changes nothing; one undo reverts exactly the composition (the op first moves the caret away and back so it cannot legitimately join an open typing run); the committed result equals what the keyboard's typing does over the same selection (final candidate typed, or the selection deleted for a cancel) — the op leaves the TYPED result in place; an arrow action never changes the model. Three `FuzzKnownDefect` entries, each tagged by the op itself: `compositionOverCrossBlockSelection`, `compositionAttributesDifferFromTyping`, `compositionJoinsEarlierUndoStep`.
- **Sweeps (new mix):** seeds 1-45 green (417.6 s; suppressed attributes ×5, undo step ×7, cross-block ×18), 46-90 green (416.4 s; ×10 / ×8 / ×22). One environmental 7.2 s op (seed 52, a Hebrew insert).
- **Endurance: no leak, no drift.** 2,000 mixed ops on one document: 101-109 MB throughout, minimum keystroke 1-7 ms without trend; 12 cycles of arrange + 100 keystrokes + Bold + undo-all flat within ±1 MB (`HarnessEnduranceTests`, a green guard). The undo stack is capped at 200 by design (`undoDepth`), so undo-all after 2,000 ops legitimately stops short of the start. **Trap:** without `autoreleasepool` per op a hosted loop grows ~5 MB per 100 keystrokes on the engine, the legacy field AND a stock `UITextView` alike — the test method's pool, not the editor.
- **Clean areas (no defect):** compositions inside bold, links, quotes, code bodies, pull quotes and authors, table cells, headings, list items and details title/body; composition then Return / Backspace / Format / undo / paste / Send (parity with a stock `UITextView`, including Return and paste REPLACING the marked text, which stock does too); two compositions in a row and typing after a composition undo like stock; emoji clusters insert, delete, copy/paste and step by arrow like stock; ⌥←, ⌘A, ⌘Z and ⇧⌘Z work with real HID keys; non-ASCII code languages round-trip (see the harness fix below).
- **Accessibility (cheap look, not filed):** the engine text view's `accessibilityValue` tracks the text (it includes a code block's language line as a line of its own); no custom actions or increment/decrement. In the hosted test process `isAccessibilityElement` is false and traits are 0, but the AX bundle is not loaded there (Tier 2 finds the engine as `app.textViews`), so that says nothing.
- **Harness fix:** `FuzzModel.describe` printed a code block's language unescaped; the Bazel log drops non-ASCII, so `lang=sw漢字` read as `lang=sw` and looked like the model losing the CJK. It is escaped now.

2026-09-24, p11 fixes (the six engine defects):

- **Fuzz entries retired**: `compositionOverCrossBlockSelection`, `compositionAttributesDifferFromTyping`, `compositionJoinsEarlierUndoStep`. With them gone the fuzz surfaced the broader attribute signature (a composition over a SELECTION attributed by the controller's marked-text rule; reduced into `HarnessCompositionFormattingTests`) and seed 29 (typing/composing into an empty quote took two undos; `HarnessCompositionUndoStepTests`), both fixed.
- **Harness fix (fuzz)**: the `compose` op closes an open run by moving the caret away and back; in an EMPTY document there is nowhere to move, so a composition after a Backspace legitimately continued that Backspace's run (stock's type-backspace-type is one step) and read as a violation (seed 29, second signature). The op now ends the run through the editor there.
- **Harness fix (fuzz)**: the `compose` op no longer demands an undo step for a composition that changed nothing (one started over a selection the gate refuses, as typing there changes nothing); the typing oracle still has to agree.
- **New per-step invariant**: `FuzzTextInputConsistency` (see the kbd row).
- **Test simulator**: a kbd abort wedges every later keyboard sync in that process and survives into later runs until the simulator is rebooted (`xcrun simctl shutdown/boot` the `BAZEL_TEST_iPhone 17_26.5` sim); a run hanging at its first `keyMove` was exactly that.
- **Extended sweep (seeds 31-90, 2026-09-24, after the fixes)**: three residuals, all "one undo did not revert the whole composition", NOT reduced: seed 58 step 26 (a cancelled Japanese composition over a cross-block selection from a quote child into the paragraph after it leaves its first candidate `k` committed; the same shape in isolation behaves, so it depends on the history — a Format ▸ Quote + edit + redo before it), seed 63 step 7 (a Korean composition over a one-character selection in a code body, after a cancelled composition over a selection and two no-op formats: one undo went back past an earlier deletion), seed 86 step 33 (a pinyin composition over a ⇧-extended selection right after typing inside a quoted code block). The default 30 seeds are green (run as 1-15 / 16-30 with a simulator reboot between, because seed 15 aborts kbd).

2026-09-24, p12 roam (dictation, Writing Tools, find, drag and drop, text replacements, live environment):

- **Six engine rows above** (theme change; attributed marked text over a selection; multi-line replacement attributes; newline + text at a heading start; newlines in one-line fields; no drag and drop). All red, with controls green.
- **What was driven, and how (Tier 1, hosted).** Dictation: `insertDictationResult(_:)` with `UIDictationPhrase`s built by KVC (`setValue(_:forKey: "text")`), in spoiler, monospace and bold runs, over cross-block selections, a table covered in part, cells, lists, headings, quotes, code bodies and languages, authors, then undo; the p11 input-delegate recorder wrapped around a dictation. The engine's `insertDictationResult:` asks the kernel (`insertDictationResultIfSupported:`), which answers NO (the dictation capability is not validated), and falls back to `insertText:` of the joined phrases. So dictation goes through the gate and the typed-text policy and matches typing everywhere except the two newline rows above. Text replacements: `replace(_:withText:)` with a two-line text over a word in every block kind, compared with typing over the same selection. Writing Tools-shaped replacements: whole-paragraph rewrites over formatted runs, across a quote, over a whole table, and inside code. Only the rows above diverged; losing a container that a rewrite wholly covers follows the gate's documented rule. Environment: resigning first responder mid-composition, then refocusing and typing; the background/foreground notification sequence; `didReceiveMemoryWarningNotification` with a table and during a composition; `UITextInputMode.currentInputModeDidChangeNotification` mid-composition; the root's `overrideUserInterfaceStyle`, `traitOverrides.preferredContentSizeCategory` (AX XXXL) and `traitOverrides.layoutDirection` (RTL) over quote, table, spoiler and code documents, with the caret, the per-step invariants and a keystroke after each; the panel theme (row above).
- **Clean (no defect):** the memory warning, the notification sequence, the input-mode notification and the three trait overrides. The composer's font, colours and direction come from presentation data, so the traits change nothing, and no drift or derived-view shortfall appeared. After a theme change, colours are right and undo/redo keep the new colours. A resign in the middle of a composition leaves the text marked, but a stock `UITextView` does the same in-process: only the keyboard daemon commits on resign. `setAttributedMarkedText` at a caret and inside spoiler, monospace and bold matches `setMarkedText`, and the marked-text underline does not leak into the model.
- **Declared, not drivable here.** Writing Tools: the engine view answers `writingToolsBehavior` = `.default` and `allowedWritingToolsResultOptions` = default. It has no `UIWritingToolsCoordinator` and no `isWritingToolsActive`, and Apple Intelligence does not run in the simulator. Find and Replace: the engine view is not `UITextSearching` and has no find interaction; the legacy view has `isFindInteractionEnabled = false`, so there is no parity gap. Dictation placeholders: the engine implements none of `insertDictationResultPlaceholder`, `removeDictationResultPlaceholder:willInsertResult:`, `frameForDictationResultPlaceholder:` or `insertTextPlaceholderWithSize:`/`removeTextPlaceholder:`, while a stock `UITextView` implements all of them (a U+FFFC placeholder with a frame). UIKit then uses no placeholder for this view; not filed, because it is optional API. Scribble: no `UIScribbleInteraction` or `UIIndirectScribbleInteraction` on either backend, and the iPhone simulator has no Pencil. Drag and drop: the row above.
- **Harness changes.** `HarnessPanelHost.updatePresentationInterfaceState(_:)` is a seam for driving the panel's theme path. The fuzz now ends every sequence with a theme round trip (dark, then light), placed after redo-all and before copy → paste-back. It consumes no randomness, so every seed's op stream is unchanged. Invariants: the document equals the tip, the settled per-step invariants hold, and the selection is kept (`FuzzKnownDefect.themeChangeMovesSelection`, the first open entry). Default 30 seeds green, run as 1-15 (148.0 s, 11 suppressions) and 16-30 (144.0 s, 13), with no other theme violation.
- **Harness gaps.**
  - `FuzzModel.describe` prints no list membership (`[p body "item one"]` for a bullet item). Model equality still compares it, but a describe-based assertion or failure message cannot see a list change. The p12 probes printed `marker/level` themselves.
  - `FuzzModel.engineLines` / `engineAxisMismatch` does not model a FOLDED details block: a freshly arranged `[details folded …]` reports an axis mismatch with no edit at all (the fuzz never folds one, so it has not bitten).
  - There is no dictation oracle: a stock `UITextView` given KVC-built phrases in the test process inserts nothing, so dictation was compared with typing instead.
  - Real resign-with-keyboard, real backgrounding and a real keyboard switch mid-composition need the keyboard daemon, which a hosted test does not drive; these were not attempted on K1, since a composition cannot be delivered through `axe`.
  - Theme: only the dark/light pair is driven. A Text Size change reaches the engine as a render-metrics push (`applyRenderMetrics` also re-sets the document, so it is likely to share the theme row's defect). It is not driven, because `ChatPresentationInterfaceState` has no `updatedFontSize` and the panel forces `baseFontSize = 17`. (p12 fixes: it did share it; now driven on the editor's `renderMetrics` directly, `HarnessThemeChangeSelectionTests.testATextSizeChangeKeepsTheSelection`.)

2026-09-24, p12 fixes (by the controller's subagent):

- **Five of the six p12 engine rows are fixed** (their "Fixed" notes above); `FuzzKnownDefect` is empty again (`themeChangeMovesSelection` retired). Default 30 seeds green in one process (290.3 s, no suppressions); extended sweep 31-60 (290.4 s) and 61-90 (281.7 s) green.
- **Two host suites pinned the old one-line-field rule** (the newline DELETED): `EngineContainerPasteTests` (2) and `EngineResolverTests` (2) now pin the space. Changed as the fix's own consequence, not weakened.
- **Drag and drop is left open, red, by decision.** What it needs, measured against the code: (1) the engine view gets a `UIDragInteraction` and a `UIDropInteraction` of its own — `UITextDragInteraction` is UIKit-private and `UITextInteraction(for: .editable)` installs neither, so adopting `UITextDraggable`/`UITextDroppable` alone changes nothing; (2) the drag's lift is a long press, which competes with the engine's selection/loupe long press, the table knobs' pans and the scroll pan (`tgid_shouldBeginScrollPanHandler`), so `itemsForBeginning` must answer only for a touch inside a non-empty selection's rects and the arbitration can be proven only with real touches (Tier 2 / K1), which a hosted test cannot synthesise; (3) the drop cannot reuse the paste handlers as they stand, because every one of them reads `UIPasteboard.general` (`refusePasteOverPartlyCoveredTable`, `pastePlainTextIntoOneLineField`, `pasteFragmentFromPasteboard`, `pasteTransformedPlainTextFromPasteboard`, `deleteCrossBlockSelectionBeforeEnginePaste`, and the engine's own `paste:`) and a drop must not write the user's clipboard. **Proposed design:** extract a `PasteSource` (fragment data / plain string / RTF / media flag) that the pasteboard and an `NSItemProvider` drop session both produce, and make the handler chain take it; the drag item carries the fragment UTI (`writeSelectionToPasteboard`'s encoding) plus plain text; the drop point becomes a collapsed global range (`closestPosition(to:)` → `globalPosition(atFlatOffset:)`), refused by the same `rangeCoversATableOnlyInPart` predicate; a LOCAL move (the session's `localContext` carries the source range) is one `recordUndoStepForStructuralChange` around delete-source + insert-at-adjusted-target on a `Document` (`applyReplacingRange`), so it is one undo step by construction; previews from the selection rects (`UIDragPreviewParameters(textLineRects:)`). Items load asynchronously, so the target must be re-validated when the load finishes.

2026-09-24, drag-and-drop wave:

- **Drag and drop implemented** (row above). `RichTextEngineEditorView+DragAndDrop.swift`: its own `UIDragInteraction` (enabled only while the focused editor has a non-empty selection) and `UIDropInteraction` on the engine view. The drag carries the fragment UTI (this process only), RTF and plain text. A drop is a paste at the drop point through `EnginePasteSource` (`+Clipboard.swift`), so the clipboard is never read or written. A local move is one `Document` replacement and one undo step, with the caret after the moved content; a move covering a table in part, or a drop inside its own source, is refused. A one-line field at either end moves through the gate, so newlines become spaces. Arbitration (`tgid_shouldBeginGestureRecognizerHandler`): inside the selection the loupe (`UIVariableDelayLoupeGesture`) is refused and the drag's lift is allowed; outside it the reverse. Taps, the range-adjustment pan and the scroll pan are never asked.
- **Tests.** Tier 1 `HarnessTextDropTests` (20). It was neuter-checked: a split undo group fails the five fragment-move cases, and a clipboard write fails the clipboard case. Tier 2 `HarnessTextDragUITests`: a real press-hold-drag moves a selected word. The controls are a long press on unselected text (lifts nothing), double tap plus the edit menu, and scrolling (including a swipe from a selection). The fuzz gained a deterministic local-move round trip at the end of each sequence; it consumes no randomness.
- **Harness facts.** A press within about 22pt of a selection handle drags the handle. "quick" is narrower than the two handle zones, so the drag test uses the new `long-word` scenario. A press straight after a double tap falls in UIKit's tap-and-a-half window and extends the selection. The legacy composer does not drag under the same XCUITest gesture, so it cannot be the control. `many-lines` is now 40 lines: at 14 the field did not scroll.

  - Also driven: oscillating through seven widths with no settle; widths of 40, 80 and 140pt, then typing; typing, Return, Backspace and select in the same run-loop turn as a resize.
