  - Keyboard height: `maxHeight` 400 → 150 → 90 → 400, with the caret at the start, middle and end and with a selection, against the legacy composer. Arrow-key navigation (↓×20, ⇧↓, ⌘↑/⌘↓, ↑, →×400) against a stock `UITextView`.
  - Accessibility: the engine view's `UIAccessibility` answers against legacy and stock, plus `axe describe-ui` on K1 for both backends.
- **Clean (no defect):** everything above except the four rows.
  - Once settled, a width change leaves the geometry and pixels identical to a fresh layout, the model and selection unchanged, no verifier or axis violation, and no `textWillChange`/`textDidChange`/`selectionDidChange` sent (stock sends none either). An open composition survives and commits where it was. Narrow widths do not crash the engine. Rotation matches.
  - When the field shrinks, the engine keeps the caret visible better than legacy (legacy lets an end caret go off the bottom).
  - Arrow keys reveal the caret vertically, at least as well as stock.
  - The engine view is exposed as a `TextArea` whose value tracks the text.
- **Observations, not filed.** The AX value reads a table as one line per cell, and a code block's empty language line as a blank line. A table in portrait is laid out with its far columns off the right edge at offset 0, which is by design (the run scrolls); finding (4) is only that nothing scrolls it to the caret.
- **Harness changes.**
  - `BUILD` plist: landscape-left and landscape-right are now supported orientations.
  - `HarnessPanelHost.maxHeight` is the keyboard-height knob (default 400, unchanged behaviour).
  - The fuzz runs a WIDTH round trip after the theme round trip in both methods (`resizeRoundTripViolation`: 300, 700, back to 402). It consumes no randomness, so every seed's stream is unchanged, and it prints `width round trip ok`.
  - Manual: a new "Width, rotation and keyboard height" section.
- **Sweeps with the round trip.** Both fuzz methods are green for seeds 1-15 (162.8 / 177.4 s), 16-30 (179.8 / 156.9 s), 31-50 (233.0 / 241.5 s) and 51-70 (210.2 / 214.6 s), with no suppressions. Every sequence reached the round trip. The Tier 2 suite is green (16) after the plist change.
- **Not drivable here.** Rotation with the software keyboard up: the Bazel simulators have a hardware keyboard, and there is no command-line rotation for K1. VoiceOver itself (rotor navigation, text-editing gestures): it needs the AX runtime on a device or with VoiceOver on. The in-process AX bundle does not activate, even after `dlopen`. Width 0 hits an AsyncDisplayKit debug assert (`-[ASDisplayNode setFrame:]` {{16, 6}, {-32, 50}}) in the PANEL layout, not in the engine; it was not pursued (not a realistic width, and not the engine).
- **p14 fixes (2026-09-24).** All four rows above fixed; `HarnessWidthChangeSelectionDisplayTests` 3/3, `HarnessAccessibilityTests` 6/6 (two new: the formula / pill readings, VoiceOver's selection on the value's axis), `HarnessTableCaretRevealTests` 7/7 (three new: a width change alone, a manual scroll survives passes that move nothing, undo into a far column). The first fuzz sweep of 31-60 after the table fix failed seed 34 in both methods at the width round trip (the reveal was measured before a redo-re-created table was placed); fixed by spending the reveal in the wrapper's layout pass and pinned as `HarnessComposerFuzzTests.testEngineFuzzSeed34WidthRoundTrip` (red with the reveal performed in the host pass, green now).

2026-09-24, p15 roam (external content sets while editing, Send in odd states, performance at scale; fuzz push op):

- **Nine engine rows above** (undo across an external set; replay rebuilds an atom paragraph; a details left with no body; table rows lag a width change; a whole-table edit into a secondary line splits model and engine; Format over N atoms is quadratic; typing across a media unit takes the end's formatting; underline lost typing inside a link; emoji after a caption entity drifts). Each is red with its controls green.
- **What was driven (Tier 1, hosted).** External sets go through `ChatPanelInterfaceInteraction.updateTextInputStateAndMode` (the `ChatControllerNode` route, which the harness host already pushes back into the panel): a different content and/or selection while the engine held a caret, a selection, an open typing run, an IME composition (plain, in a table cell, in a code block), a lifted drag (`makeTextDragContext` then `performLocalMove`), and with the caret in a table cell, code, a caption; content-equal pushes with another selection; rapid alternating pushes with typing between; a push from inside the editor's own `onChange` (a wrapping interaction that rewrites `:)` → 😀 synchronously); out-of-range pushed selections. Send (`simulateSend`) with marked text, a selection, after undo, mid-drag, in code (input traits). Performance: typing, caret moves, undo / redo, Select All ▸ Bold / Spoiler / Quote, copy, delete-all over tables to 40×10, 80 media, 500 list items, 30 nested quotes, 200 custom emoji, 800-line code; memory over 1000 undo/redo cycles.
- **Clean (no defect):** a push drops an open composition cleanly (no leak into the next candidate or commit; stock and legacy do the same), lands the pushed selection, resets code input traits both ways, keeps the drawn cursor and highlight in step, reveals the pushed caret in a long document; a reentrant push from `onChange` lands and is undoable; a content-equal push keeps the focused editor's selection (by design, `echoedSelectionShouldApply`; legacy applies it); a push mid-drag makes the drop a copy (the `documentAtLift` guard); Send with marked text sends the candidate (legacy the same) and leaves nothing marked; typing after Send / a push is plain.
- **Observations, not filed.** (1) Caret-move cost is linear in the document and ~10× legacy's: 45 ms per arrow at 400 paragraphs (legacy 4 ms), independent of the chat wiring; typing is cheaper than legacy there (54 vs 168 ms). (2) Select All ▸ Bold / Quote over plain paragraphs and lists grows a little faster than linear (400 paragraphs Bold 0.70 s, 480 list items Quote 1.7 s). (3) An external set of DIFFERENT content replaces the whole engine document (the chat's content has fresh model ids), so a draft push to a long document costs a full load (0.2-0.3 s at 400 paragraphs) — expected for an external set.
- **Harness artifact (not a defect):** 1000 undo/redo cycles grew the process by ~1.9 GB when timed in a tight loop — the autoreleased objects of `settle(0.0)` are never drained inside one test method. With each cycle in `autoreleasepool {}` the footprint is flat (116 MB over 500 cycles, likewise for 500 host sets and 500 type/delete pairs). Measure memory with a pool per iteration.
- **Fuzz.** A new `push` op (`FuzzOp.push`, `FuzzPush`, `pushViolation`, `nextPush`), drawn from its own stream at 7 % of steps, on in `runFuzz` (both methods) and off in the seed-34 pin. Undo-all may now also stop at any pushed model. Six explicit `FuzzKnownDefect` entries: `undoCrossesExternalSet`, `detailsWithoutBody`, `tableRowsLagWidth`, `wholeTableToSecondaryLine` (corrupting), `typingIntoCaption`, `captionEntityEmojiDrift`. Sweeps: on the final code default 1-30 (352 s) and edited content 1-30 (396 s) green; while the entries were being added, default 31-60, 61-90, 91-120 and edited 31-60, 61-90, 91-120 each went green once the entry for what they found was in (later changes only added entries). Suppression counts per 30 seeds: `undoCrossesExternalSet` ~80-90, the others 1-3.
- **Not driven.** A real drag session in flight (the drop's entry points were driven directly); the software keyboard's own behaviour across a push (Bazel's simulators have a hardware keyboard); pushes from the real chat's draft / suggestion machinery (the harness has no input-query pipeline).

2026-09-25, p15 fix wave: all nine p15 engine rows fixed (root cause and fix in each row). The six p15

check now requires landing EXACTLY on the model the last external set left (the arrange, a Send that
cleared, a content-changing push) — it used to accept the start or any pushed model, which is where an
undo across a set lands. Sweeps with no suppressions: default and edited content, seeds 1-30 / 31-60 /

case, and the harness readout's drawn label is capped (its accessibility value is not).

2026-09-25, p15 follow-up (Format over media; the chat's own edits and the undo stack): four rows above.

- **Format over media blocks was not quadratic any more.** Its 9.4-11.2x was the timed window's
  `settle(0.0)` letting a Core Animation commit land in some samples; Bold over media is ~4x in either
  mode. The profile (probes + `sample`) did find three real quadratic terms and two more passes behind
  the flagged open risk: the spoiler decorator's block walk (every caret move), the canvas's per-block
  re-insert, `flatOffset` per block in `rederiveAtomPresentation` / `normalisePresentation`, and per-run
  merges in `rerenderPresentation` (a theme change over 240 paragraphs: 8.4 s) and
  `normalisePresentation` (Monospace off: 14.6 s). New pins: `HarnessCaretMoveScalingTests`,
  `HarnessPresentationPassScalingTests`; the timing rule is in the manual.
- **The chat's own edits.** Classified every content-changing push into `setInputContent`: the user's
  edits through the chat all arrive through `updateTextInputStateAndMode` and are now one undo step
  (`HarnessChatSideEditUndoTests`, 10 paths); the editor's own commands (Format, ⌘B, link, quote /
  code / pull quote, paste) never reach it; Send's clear, draft sync / restore and edit-message loads
  arrive through `updateChatPresentationInterfaceState` and keep clearing. The harness now drives
  external sets through `HarnessPanelHost.setInputContent(_:selectionRange:)` (tests and the fuzz
  `push` op), not through the interaction.
- **Runs.** Scaling classes four times in a row, all ratios 2.9-4.2. Full Tier 1: 460 tests, failures
  only the 13 legacy-by-design ones; Tier 2 16/0; fuzz default and edited 1-60 `failing seeds []`, no
  known-defect entries; host suites 1455/5, 1065/0, 127/0; SwiftPM Core 463/1.
- **Observation, not filed.** A caret move still re-decorates EVERY block (the decoration is marked
  dirty on each selection change, spoiler or not): linear now, ~19 ms at 300 paragraphs, 67 ms at 1200.
  Marking it dirty only when a spoiler's reveal can change would take it off the caret path.

Format: Quote / Code / Pull Quote / Bold / Spoiler / Monospace / Italic over selections starting or ending at a paragraph boundary, the break alone, empty paragraphs, list items; ⇧⌘N over mixed formatting + undo / redo; legacy Quote / Code as the control. RTL: an Arabic paragraph's caret geometry, word / character / line moves over mixed Latin-Hebrew-digits and inside an RTL paragraph, caret x at every offset and selection rects over bidi runs, all against a stock `UITextView`; Bold / Italic / Spoiler over mixed runs, Quote, set → get; RTL text in a list item, quote, pull quote (with an RTL author) and code body, with Return and typing.

Tab indents the FIRST item of a list and has no depth limit (level 9 reached). Return in the MIDDLE of a checked item leaves the tail unchecked. A heading's partial copy pastes as a heading block (`isInlineMergeable`'s documented decision for whole headings reaches a partial copy too).
- **Fuzz** (`HarnessComposerFuzzTests`): a `line` op (`FuzzLineOp`, 8 % of steps from its own stream, on in `runFuzz` only, so the op stream and the seed-34 pin are unchanged): seed a checklist by paste, the checkbox tap (exact flip + undo / redo), Return at a checked item's start, whole-line Backspace, whole-line block Format. New per-step invariant: no bare U+FFFC in the model. Six `FuzzKnownDefect` entries: `checkedItemReturnAtStart`, `wholeLineDeletionRestyles`, `blockCommandSelectionEnd` (op signatures), and `pullQuoteAtomPaste`, `codeFlattensAtoms`, `cellReplaceFlattensAtoms` (bare-U+FFFC by container, corrupting). With the bare-U+FFFC invariant the first full sweep (1-90) failed edited seeds 12, 32, 38, 56, 80, all reduced into `HarnessAtomFlatteningTests`; default 1-90 green. Sweeps with the line ops before the bare-U+FFFC invariant: default 1-30 green (237 s), edited 1-30 green (241 s), default 31-90 green (476 s), edited 31-90 failed seed 83 only (below and the pull-quote row).
- **Harness fix (fuzz invariant):** seed 83's first failure was `FuzzTextInputConsistency`'s "line boundary forward from the selection start is on the wrong side" after ⇧↑ landed on a line start inside a pull quote (right after a U+2028). A stock `UITextView` answers exactly the same (the separator, one before the anchor), so the invariant now forgives that one answer; `HarnessLineSeparatorTokenizerOracleTests` pins stock's answer and the engine's agreement.
- **Harness change (scaling timing):** `HarnessNestedQuoteKeystrokeScalingTests` and `HarnessLargeRichPasteScalingTests` now time the command plus a synchronous `root.view.layoutIfNeeded()`, with `CATransaction.flush()` outside the window and an `autoreleasepool` per sample (the paste test had timed `paste:` alone). After: nested ratio 1.8 (twice), paste ratio 3.3. Neuter check: the nested test goes red (5.6) with `authorLinePresenceThatIsWrong` building each quote's span before the `seen` filter again; the paste test did NOT go red with `role(forEngineIdentity:)`'s array lookup and the canvas's `self.subviews.count` restored together (3.4) — the third fixed term (the projection cache) was not neutered, so its ability to catch a regression of those two alone is unproven.

2026-09-25, p18 roam (the p17 fix wave's two open leads, chat-side edits, autocomplete in structured blocks):

- **Two engine rows above.** (1) The chat's emoji-keyboard insert and the avatar menu's Mention rebuild the composer through `ChatTextInputState(inputText:)`; CORRUPTING. (2) A paste-back duplicates a trailing empty pull quote or code block. Both are red, with their controls green, in two runs each.
- **Lead (a), the p17 seed-28 flake: harness timing, not a product defect.** UIKit's plain-text paste is ASYNCHRONOUS in the engine and in a stock `UITextView` alike. Measured: it lands 20-30 ms after `paste:`, after 6-9 run-loop spins. A content set made in that window is overtaken by the paste in both, with `canUndo` true in both. The fuzz's step settle (0.02 s) was shorter than the paste, so the next op (seed 28: a push) ran first. The markdown / rich paste paths are synchronous. **Harness fix:** every paste op spins `FuzzConfig.pasteSettle` (0.25 s). `HarnessPasteLatencyOracleTests` (2, green) pins engine = stock.
- **Lead (b), deferred host sets inside a composition: clean.** 758 compositions (Japanese and Korean), checking at every candidate that the marked text is still the candidate, then comparing the commit with typing. They covered every caret and selection of ten list documents: bullets, ordered, checked, nested, quoted lists, Arabic and Hebrew items, and an RTL paragraph before a list. No divergence. The list-continuation reconciliation never fired mid-composition in any of them (the p17 sibling-only rule holds). The engine's other deferred work touches no document: the coalesced `onChange`, selection presentation, caret-reveal audit and autoscroll. A syntax highlighter is registered but not consumed by the engine. The chat's deferred custom-emoji resolution already updates the model in place (`ChatInterfaceInputContexts.swift`), and content equality turns its push into an echo.
- **Autocomplete in structured blocks (driven, no engine defect).** Mention, hashtag and emoji-search completion went through the chat's own transforms (`replacingFlatRange`) in code, pull quotes, quotes, lists and headings. Structure is kept, there is no verifier drift, and get → set → get is stable. Observations, not filed: (1) Nothing suppresses suggestions in a code block, so a mention ENTITY is stored in a code block's runs (`[code "let "+[e]"Alice"]`). Whether code should suppress them is a product question, and legacy's code block is not suppressed either. (2) A quote's or pull quote's author line and a table cell are off the flat axis, so no query is computed there.
- **Not driven / out of scope.** Markdown autoformat while typing (`# `, `- `, `**…**`): neither backend has it. Draft save/restore and edit-message load are out of scope by decision (the rich edit load uses `ChatTextInputState(content:)`, lossless). Accessibility and drag-and-drop were covered by p14 and the drag-and-drop wave. Format ▸ Date on the engine is a documented no-op (`RichTextEditorChatInputNode.performFormatAction(.date)`, TODO).
- **Fuzz.** New `chatInsert` op (5 %, its own stream, `runFuzz` only), so every other stream and the seed-34 pin are unchanged. Two known-defect entries, both non-ending: `chatInsertRebuildsStructure` (the op leaves the typed result) and `emptyTrailingContainerDuplicated` (recognised at the paste-back). Sweeps on the final code, `failing seeds []` everywhere. Default 1-45: 515.6 s, chatInsert ×86, emptyTrailing ×1. Edited 1-45: 528.6 s, ×100 / ×1. Default 46-90: 517.1 s, ×95. Edited 46-90: 520.0 s, ×106.
- **Chat paths audited** (every `ChatTextInputState(inputText:)` built from an existing state). The emoji keyboard and the context-menu Mention are filed above. Format ▸ Date's trailing `withUpdatedEffectiveInputState(ChatTextInputState(inputText:…))` and the legacy link branch are unreachable on the engine (Date is a no-op there; the link has an engine branch). The command menu's fallback builds fresh text. Translate without a selection replaces everything by design.

(seed 47) and italic moved into a code language line drifted (seed 53). All fixed. Both p18

still compares the full model (formatting included).

UITextInput conformance, a long session):

- **Eight engine rows above** (characterRange past an edge raises; three selection shapes with no geometry;
  Cut into a caption duplicates the medium; a move does not join its source's halves; a folded quote's AX
  reading; a paste into a table cell deletes the table; a move destroys content). Last run of the new
  classes: 24 red, 20 controls green.
- **(a) Default seed 80, failing only inside a batch: harness timing, not a leak.** `.line(.seedChecklist)`
  pasted without `FuzzConfig.pasteSettle`. At seed 80 step 34 the caret is not in a top-level body
  paragraph, so the markdown path declines and UIKit's own paste lands asynchronously; the next op was a
  Japanese composition. Replayed (ops 0-33, then the paste): no settle → "one undo did not revert the whole
  japanese composition" every time; 0.02 s and 0.25 s → clean. A stock `UITextView` that composes under
  its own pending paste also ends wrong (`漢字open…`, one undo leaves `かんじ`). A slower (batched) process
  is what pushed the paste past the 0.02 s step settle. Fixed in the fuzz (the paste waits); nothing is
  shared between seeds that needed resetting.
- **(b) The one-off Tier 2 "crashed".** On the Tier 2 simulator's log: launchd `exit reason (namespace: 19
  code: 0x1) - OS_REASON_FOUNDATION, ran for 5615ms` at 19:28:30 — an orderly exit (the app cancelled its
  TCP connections first), no signal, no fault logged by the app, no terminate request from SpringBoard or
  the runner, hence no `.ips`. Three cold full UI runs (simulator shut down before each): 16/0, 16/0, 16/0.
  Not reproduced, cause unknown; the manual now says how to read the exit reason. The same log shows the
  harness is NOT hermetic: it connects to Telegram's data centres within seconds of launch.
- **What was driven.**
  - UITextInput conformance over the 11 default and 7 edited starting documents plus an RTL one: every
    offset (relative positions, `position(from:in:offset:)` and `characterRange(byExtending:)` against a
    stock `UITextView`, `caretRect` → `closestPosition` round trip, `baseWritingDirection`), every pair of
    offsets in documents up to 90 characters (`text(in:)`, `firstRect`, `selectionRects`, farthest
    positions, reversed ranges), `characterRange(at:)` at every caret. UIKit's own edge deletes
    (`_deleteForwardAndNotify:`, `_deleteByWord`, `_deleteToEndOfLine`, `_deleteForwardByWord`,
    `_transpose`, `_deleteToEndOfParagraph`) on the engine against stock.
  - Accessibility: `accessibilityValue` against the model and engine text for every starting document plus
    a folded quote and a folded details; VoiceOver's selection mapped both ways at every engine offset.
  - Text replacement (the spell checker's / keyboard's `replace(_:withText:)` over the tokenizer's word
    range) next to custom emoji, formulas, pills, mixed bold, a mention and a partly spoilered word,
    against typing over the same range and stock word ranges.
  - Drag and drop: 220 random local moves over the starting documents against Cut + Paste; then the new
    fuzz op.
  - A long session: 1,500 ops of the whole mix on one editor, each op in its own autorelease pool.
- **Clean (no defect):** positions, relative offsets, left/right steps and `characterRange(byExtending:)`
  away from the edges match stock; caret round trips, `text(in:)` on every range, farthest positions and
  reversed ranges are consistent everywhere; `accessibilityValue` and VoiceOver's selection round-trip at
  every engine offset (apart from the folded-quote row); text replacement equals typing over the same
  range in every case, and stock's word ranges match (an attachment character is its own word in both);
  typing and composing in a 2,000-character paragraph cost 4 ms / 7 ms per keystroke (many short
  paragraphs 11 ms); the long session held 107-118 MB (140 MB at a 1,200-character peak) with no trend,
  100 ops per 10-24 s.
- **Observations, not filed:**
  - A block nested ~20 quotes deep has no text column left (seed 10: 21 levels, every caret in the block at
    one point); a selection touching it has no geometry, because one line with no extent fails the whole
    answer. Legacy's quote also floors its column (`max(…, 1)`). The fuzz's geometry invariant exempts a
    selection touching a line with no extent.
  - Moves and Cut + Paste disagree in how an inserted pull-quote or language-line fragment is shaped (the
    move inserts it inline, the paste as a block); both defensible, not filed.
  - The long session saw a Korean `.insertFinal` composition whose commit was italic where typing the
    same text was plain (after 1,219 ops, in an empty document), and two others in 1,000+-character
    documents; not reduced.
- **Fuzz changes.** `move` op (5 %, own stream, `runFuzz` only; `moveViolation`: one undo step both ways,
  no atom or letter created or lost); selection-geometry and edge-extension checks in
  `FuzzTextInputConsistency` (exemptions name their red tests); `FuzzKnownDefect.moveLosesContent`
  (corrupting, ends the sequence); `seedChecklist` waits for its paste; `atomCount` counts atoms, not
  merged runs.
- **Sweeps (final code):** default 1-45 `failing seeds []` (moveLosesContent x5, 493 s), 46-90 `[]` (x2,
  558 s); edited 1-45 `[]` (x6, 540 s), 46-90 `[]` (x6, 505 s). Before the move filter: edited 1-45
  `[8, 10, 39]`, 46-90 `[54, 56, 57, 67, 84]` (all moves, plus seed 10's collapsed column); before the
  geometry exemptions, default 1-45 `[9, 10, 44]`. Earlier in the roam, default 70-80 and 46-90 (without
  the new checks) were green, i.e. seed 80 did not recur in either batch.

2026-09-26, p19 fix wave:

- **All nine p19 red classes green** (24 red → 0), plus one new filing found by the wave's own sweep
  (`HarnessQuoteAuthorCutMoveTests`, 3 cases, fixed). Root causes in each row above.
- **`FuzzKnownDefect.moveLosesContent` retired**; the three `FuzzTextInputConsistency` geometry exemptions
  (atom break, lone soft break, mark-led block) removed; `characterRange(byExtending:)` is now checked at the
  document edges too. The one exemption kept is a line with no extent (seed 10's collapsed column), which
  names no red test.
- **Fuzz letter invariant for moves** leaves code LANGUAGE lines out (edited seed 33): a moved piece of a
  code block is a code block carrying its language, as Copy carries it, so a move into a block destination
  legitimately adds a language line. A move that would LOSE a language (into a cell, edited seed 57) is
  refused instead.
- **Sweeps (final code):** default 1-45 `failing seeds []` (544 s), 46-90 `[]` (550 s); edited 1-45 `[]`
  (553 s), 46-90 `[]` (561 s); no known-defect entries. Before the author/language fixes: edited 1-45 `[33]`,
  46-90 `[57]`, and the default suite's 1-30 `[27]` / edited `[19, 27]`.
- **The Korean `.insertFinal`-committed-italic observation is the ORACLE, not the engine.** Reduced: Italic
  toggled at a caret in an empty document (a pending typing attribute), then a composition. The commit is
  italic, and so is TYPING the same text straight after the toggle (measured); the fuzz's oracle types
  after one undo, which drops the pending attribute as a stock text view's undo does, and so types plain.
  Both commits (`.unmark` too) show it. Not filed; if the fuzz meets it again, the oracle should re-apply
  the pending typing attributes before typing.
- **Hermetic harness.** A loopback SOCKS5 proxy (`127.0.0.1:1`) written before the account opens; the Tier 2
  simulator's log went from 34,152 data-centre lines in one pre-fix hour to 0 over a full UI run's 20
  launches (manual, "The harness is hermetic").


2026-09-30, p20 roam (the send-path oracle, external paste sources, p19 leftovers):

- **Thirteen rows above** — three send-path losses (formatting inside code, a blank line between carved
  blocks on an edit reload, a spoilered standalone formula), six external-paste defects (an image as a
  bare `U+FFFC`, foreign colours kept in storage, two undos over a cross-block paste, a paste joining the
  typing run, carriage returns kept, a medium destroyed by a multi-line paste at its stop), a crash (an
  edit ending inside an emoji modifier sequence in an author line), the deep-quote column, a no-op move in a
  pull quote registering no undo step, and a Format over half a surrogate pair. Six corrupt what is sent;
  one is a crash.
- **The send-path oracle** (`Tests/HarnessSendPathOracle.swift`). What the chat SENDS for a model, loaded
  back as message editing loads it, must be the model modulo a documented canonical form (trim, plain
  text's single kind of line break, emoji file / animation flag, list level, author ambient bold / italic,
  table widths, media geometry, an empty code block or single-line empty quote as an empty line). Every
  step is a shared function; the loop's glue in `ChatControllerNode.sendCurrentMessage` is mirrored and
  should move into a linked module. Clean on every default and edited starting document and ~50 stress
  documents apart from the rows: nested and overlapping formatting, entity offsets (UTF-16), custom emoji
  ids, dates, mentions, links over emoji, RTL marks, `U+2028`, tables, details (folded and expanded), code
  languages, pull-quote and quote authors (entities too), checklists, media captions, trailing empty
  blocks, markdown-literal text (converted by design), and a Postbox round trip of the rich attribute.
- **Observations, not filed:** (1) `generateChatInputTextEntities` merges touching `BlockQuote` entities
  only for the FIRST quote (`break scan`), so a second quote split by inline formatting is sent as several
  touching `BlockQuote` entities; this client's decoder and renderer compare quote attributes by value and
  show one quote, other clients unverified. (2) An external set whose text holds `U+2028` leaves the chat's
  copy with `U+2028` and the node's model with `\n` until the next edit (same length, harmless). (3) A
  date entity's `format` is not in the model, so editing a message with a formatted date re-sends it
  unformatted. (4) Plain text's soft and hard breaks are one kind on the text route (canonicalised).
- **External pastes, driven clean apart from the rows:** RTF / RTFD / HTML / URL / two text items into an
  empty composer, a paragraph, a word, a heading, a list item (membership kept), a quote child, code, the
  language line, a cell, a caption (a multi-line paste splits it, the documented Return rule), a pull-quote
  body (italic inert by design) and author, a details title (one-line fields take the plain text), and
  over a cross-block selection; bold / italic / underline / strikethrough / links map into the model, no
  foreign font SIZE survives. The first HTML import in a process lands ~1 s late (WebKit), which a short
  settle mistakes for a duplicated paste — harness timing (manual updated).
- **p19 leftovers.** (a) The collapsed column is an engine defect (row above, depths 1-30 measured).
  (b) Moves are Cut + Paste: 135 random moves over the default and edited documents, 0 disagreements.
  (c) `_caretRectForOffset:` → `caretRectForPosition:`: neither the engine view nor its behaviour input
  responds to `_caretRectForOffset:`, and the public edges (`caretRect(for: endOfDocument)`,
  `scrollRectToVisible`) are clean; not reachable as far as measured.
- **Fuzz changes.** Per-step: the chat's copy equals the node's model in full, and the send-path oracle
  (on the chat's copy); end of sequence: the edit load done for real. New `externalPaste` op (5 %, own
  stream, `runFuzz` only; one undo / redo round trip). Known-defect entries, each recognised by its exact
  shape: `sendPathLoss(.codeRunFormattingDropped | .blankLineBetweenCarvesDropped |
  .standaloneFormulaSpoilerDropped)`, `pasteJoinsTypingRun`, `pasteAtMediaStop` (corrupting),
  `noOpMoveRegistersNoStep`.

  stable), monotonic over 300-420pt in 3pt steps with no oscillation, model unchanged, caret preserved, a
  typing run continuing across a width change is still one undo step, redo across a width change restores.

Full `ComposerHarnessTests` 769 run, only the 13 legacy-by-design tests failing; `ComposerHarnessUITests` 16/0.

2026-10-02, p23 roam (the p22 crash, neuter checks of the p22 tests, p22's open observations, entities):

- **The p22 crash, reduced.** `Fatal error: N engine blocks against N-1 roles` (`TableLayout.swift:65`) is a code
  language or a quote / pull-quote author holding a newline, rendered raw (row above,
  `HarnessNewlineInOneLineFieldCrashTests`). It is the only way into that trap found here: a set of such
  content, or `U+2028` delivered by `insertText:` / a composition / the chat's insert, followed by any
  re-render (theme, undo / redo, Send + edit). Paste and drop flatten it. Default seed 73 is clean alone, as the
  p22 wave said. The check is an `assertionFailure`, so it is compiled out of a release build. There it
  recovers nothing: the roles stay shifted and the message loses its last block (measured with the assertion
  temporarily disabled). It should become a rebuild of the roles from the document, not a fatal trap or a
  quiet return.
- **Neuter checks of the p22 classes** (each fix neutered, rebuilt, restored and `cmp`-verified). Every case
  that was red before its fix goes red again. Neighbour cases that STAY GREEN under their neuter:
  `HarnessRedoDuringCompositionTests` `testACompositionWithNoRedoKeepsTheUndoStack`,
  `testUndoDuringACompositionOverARedo` (side-effect guards); `HarnessAuthoredQuoteWideRangeTests`
  `testTheResultIsStableThroughASet`, `testUndoRestoresTheQuote` (they pass whatever shape the edit leaves);
  `HarnessQuoteBodyIntoAuthorRangeTests` `testCopyOfARangeIntoTheAuthorCarriesTheAuthorSlice`,
  `testCutAndPasteOfARangeIntoTheAuthorLosesNothing` (red only with the one-line-field fix neutered too);
  `HarnessFragmentIntoOneLineFieldTests` `testACodeBodySlicePastedIntoAFieldIsTheSliceAlone`,
  `testAMoveOfACodeBodySliceIntoItsLanguage` (pin the fix wave's first wrong version, and green with the
  explicit one-line flatten neutered as well), and `testAMoveOfACodeBlockWithALanguageIntoACellLosesNothing`,
  which CANNOT FAIL (its range starts inside the table cell; see the code-language row).
  `HarnessFormatOverEmptyLineSelectionTypingTests`: every non-control red.
- **p22's open observations.** (a) is filed (code language into a runs-only region). (b) a range STARTING in
  a block quote's author merges the next block's tail into the author: not filed, it is the details-title
  rule and loses no letter; a pull quote's author does NOT merge (the tail stays a paragraph), a divergence
  noted for whoever owns the rule. (c) a unit pasted into a one-line field contributes nothing: not filed. A
  move there is refused, a paste is like an image pasted into a text field, and the clipboard keeps it.
- **Driven clean (Priority 4).** Mentions, dates and links edited character by character (typing inside, at
  either end, Backspace inside, a part or the whole replaced, all characters deleted then typing, a deletion
  across the end, Cut / Copy and paste, a composition inside, undo, Return inside, Bold / Spoiler over part of
  one, Code block over one): each entity grows and shrinks as typed, and the send path loads back what was
  sent. Spoilers: typing / composing inside and at the ends, Return inside, copy of spoiler text (kept),
  spoiler across blocks then join / toggle off / type. Custom emoji: 10 in a row deleted one per Backspace, a
  selection of 3 deleted, typing between, copy / paste, Bold over them, Spoiler over 3 then Backspace; 40
  emoji-only, Cut + Paste and Return in the middle. Policy differences from legacy, not filed: typing over a
  WHOLE selected mention / date / link keeps the entity (legacy drops it; a stock text view keeps it), and
  typing at an entity's start does not join it (legacy does).
- **Fuzz.** A new `oneLineInsert` op and two per-step invariants, plus three open `FuzzKnownDefect` entries,
  each naming its red test: `newlineInOneLineField` (corrupting), `inlineCodeOverEntity`,
  `linkExtentDependsOnUndoHistory`. The op counts its hit and undoes it at once, then clears redo with a
  letter typed and deleted. Ending the sequence instead cut most seeds short (sweep A, abandoned), and leaving
  the undone step redoable let the fuzz's own `.redo` trap the process (sweep B, default seed 3). The disk
  filled during sweep C (the test simulator's symbolication cache: see the manual).
- **Sweeps (final, D):** default 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[48]`, 61-90 `[]`; edited 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[]`, 61-90
  `[]`. Counted: `inlineCodeOverEntity` x1246, `newlineInOneLineField` x241, `linkExtentDependsOnUndoHistory`
  x2. **Default seed 48 is NOT reduced.** It fails in the end-of-sequence edit reload: a paragraph that is two
  adjacent identical custom emoji (`[p [e]"😀😀"]`, made by the chat's custom-emoji insert next to an existing
  one, step 55) shows ONE attachment on the engine axis after the reload, where the model holds two. Direct
  probes of two adjacent same-id custom emoji through both send routes and the edit load reload as two;
  one custom-emoji RUN spanning two emoji renders one attachment and keeps "😀😀" in the model. So the
  seed-48 path somewhere merged the pair into one run. Replay: `FUZZ_SEEDS=48 FUZZ_VERBOSE=1`.
- **Last run of the new classes (one run, 4 classes, 32 tests):** `HarnessNewlineInOneLineFieldCrashTests` 8 red
  (7 TRAP, 1 fails) / 3 green; `HarnessCodeLanguageIntoRunsRegionTests` 4 / 4; `HarnessInlineCodeOverEntityTests`
  5 / 3; `HarnessCompositionAfterLinkFromLineEndTests` 2 / 3.

2026-10-02, p23 fix wave: all four p23 rows fixed, plus the unreduced default seed 48 (reduced and filed as its own
row: the fuzz's normaliser merged two adjacent identical custom emoji, and the composer's bridge turned a
two-emoji run into one atom; both ends fixed). Decisions: a one-line field flattens every line-break character
to one space on every door (render, read-back, gate, settle), and the layout's role-count mismatch now rebuilds
the roles from the engine's identities (debug assertion kept); a covered code language reaches a runs-only paste
through the copy's covered-text twin (the move refusal into those regions stays); Monospace over a link and Link
over inline code are refused, and content arriving with both keeps the entity; an attribute transform gives a
non-empty block's separator its last character's attributes (typing over a selection from a linked line's end
is LINKED, stock's answer); a set the editor normalises is reported back to the chat. The three p23
`FuzzKnownDefect` entries and their recognisers are retired; none is open. Sweeps with NO known-defect
entries: default 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[]`, 61-90 `[]`; edited 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[]`, 61-90 `[]`, no `Fatal error`.
Full `ComposerHarnessTests` 807 run, only the 13 legacy-by-design tests failing, no traps;
`ComposerHarnessUITests` 16/0.

2026-10-02, p24 roam (audit of the p23 fix wave; length limits; new fuzz state classes):

- **Seven engine / chat rows above**, each red with passing controls: the avatar menu's Mention inside / after inline
  code loses its entity (CORRUPTING; a regression of the p23 Link refusal); set normalisations the composer does not
  report (CORRUPTING on the rich route: bold in a code body is sent); a custom emoji / formula set into a code body is
  drawn as an atom and sent as text; the length counter and edit guard ignore code blocks; CR / CRLF / `U+2029`
  inside a block (kept from a set; the paragraph tokenizer answers on the wrong side; `U+2029` reaches the model by a
  plain paste); a composition whose marked text holds "\n" splits an author / details title; a code language's edge
  space trimmed by the read-back (a theme change moves the caret; model offsets go a character off).
- **Audit of the p23 fixes.** (a) One-line fields: render, read-back, gate and settle flatten every break for typing,
  pasting, sets and compositions with `U+2028` / `U+2029` / CR (RTL authors and a whitespace-only result — a single
  space — included); a composition with "\n" in its marked text escapes (row above). The deferred report does not
  race: a set followed by another set in the same turn, typing in the next turn, a set through the user's route
  (`performUndoableInputChange`: one undo step reverting it, redo restoring it) and undo after the report all agree
  (the report pushes a content-equal state, no undo step). A Send in the same turn as a set would read the
  pre-normalisation copy, but no chat path does that. What is NOT covered is the list itself
  (`holdsContentTheEditorNormalises`, row above). (b) `recoverRolesFromEngineIdentities`, exercised by neutering the
  language render's flatten, the bridge backstop and the assertion (restored, `cmp`-verified): no trap, every
  engine block is read and no TEXT is lost from what is sent ("end" survives, which the pre-p23 release lost), but
  the recovered roles are POSITIONAL, not by identity: `[code lang="x\ny" "body"]` reads `[code lang="x" "y"] [p
  "body"]`; next to a table the cells shift (`[table ("body" | "c1")] [p "c2"]`); inside an authored quote the last
  child becomes the author and the author a bold body paragraph. The setter records identities positionally after
  the split, so "rebuilt by identity" rebuilds from an already-shifted table. Unreachable since p23 (no path into
  the mismatch found), so not filed; structure is not recovered, only text. (c) Covered-text flavour: pasting the
  editor's own copy into a paragraph, a quote child and author, a list item, a heading, a details title / body, a
  cell, a caption and a pull quote's body / author, from seven source shapes (whole / sliced code, authored quote,
  list + heading, details, pull quote, emoji + mention runs), equals the fragment-only paste everywhere except the
  three intended runs-only cases of a code-body SLICE (no language). The external plain / RTF flavours are
  untouched. Observation, not filed: a drag's item provider carries only the fragment, RTF and plain text, so a drop
  from ANOTHER engine editor in the process (not a local move) pastes a body slice's metadata language as text into a
  cell or a one-line field (single-scene app: two engine editors are not on screen at once). (d) Inline code vs
  entity: mixed selections are refused whole; markdown pastes of `` `@mention` ``, a code URL, a linked code span
  give no code+entity run; the Link prompt over inline code appears and applies nothing (UX note); the avatar menu's
  Mention into code is the row above. (e) Per-character atoms: ZWJ, keycap, flag and skin-tone alt texts stay one
  atom; a two-flag / two-emoji run splits as intended; a run whose text is two letters becomes two emoji (one entity
  over two graphemes is ambiguous with a coalesced pair; policy, not filed); an EMPTY alt text becomes a
  `U+FFFC`-text emoji (no producer known). `HarnessAtomFormatScalingTests` ratios 3.0 / 4.1 / 4.0 / 3.1 for 4x atoms.
  (f) Endurance: alone, 16 cycles stay at ~95 MB footprint / ~37 MB malloc (no per-cycle growth; the cycle touches
  none of the p23 retaining candidates). The p23 failure was a single late-suite step; the test now judges per cycle
  (manual), neuter-checked both ways: a 2 MB-per-cycle retention fails (22 MB without its largest step), a one-off
  70 MB step passes.
- **Driven clean.** Message length on the text route with mentions and custom emoji (counted as sent); heading +
  list from markdown paste (the parser gives a heading; a SET of a heading list item sends without the list —
  set-only, not filed); a ragged table, a collapsed quote, whitespace-only and RTL authors through a set.
- **Fuzz.** `chatMention` and `normalisingSet` ops, a per-step length check and a per-step language-edge check
  (manual). Harness fix: `.send` records an external set by the node's raw guard (default / edited seed 13). Six open
  `FuzzKnownDefect` cases, each naming its red test.
- **Sweeps (final, all p24 checks on):** default 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[]`, 61-90 `[]`; edited 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[]`
  (rerun: the first attempt lost edited 31-60 to a `backboardd` SIGTRAP in the test simulator at 08:54, no app
  fault), 61-90 `[]`; no `Fatal error`. Counted per 30 seeds (default / edited): `lengthIgnoresCodeBlocks` ~170-290,
  `unreportedSetNormalisation` ~30, `setTextInputInconsistent` 1-8, `setKeepsWhatIsNotSent` 2-7,
  `mentionIntoInlineCodeLosesEntity` 1-5, `languageEdgeWhitespace` 2-6. Edited seeds 69 and 80 failed before the
  language-edge check existed (both reduced to the language row).
- **Last run of the new classes (one run with ten neighbours, 18 classes, 126 tests, exactly the 24 red cases
  failing, no trap):** `HarnessMentionIntoInlineCodeTests` 3 red / 2 green, `HarnessUnreportedSetNormalisationTests`
  6 / 3, `HarnessAtomInSetCodeBodyTests` 3 / 2, `HarnessLengthCounterCodeBlockTests` 2 / 3,
  `HarnessSetCarriageReturnTests` 5 / 3, `HarnessCompositionNewlineInOneLineFieldTests` 2 / 2,
  `HarnessCodeLanguageEdgeWhitespaceTests` 3 / 2, `HarnessEnduranceTests` 1 green; neighbours green:
  `HarnessNewlineInOneLineFieldCrashTests` 11, `HarnessCodeLanguageIntoRunsRegionTests` 8,
  `HarnessInlineCodeOverEntityTests` 8, `HarnessCompositionAfterLinkFromLineEndTests` 7,
  `HarnessAdjacentCustomEmojiRunTests` 4, `HarnessAtomFormatScalingTests` 4, `HarnessFragmentIntoOneLineFieldTests`
  14, `HarnessCustomEmojiIntoCodeLanguageTests` 3, `HarnessChatInsertStructureTests` 12, `HarnessChatSideEditUndoTests` 13.

2026-10-02, p24 fix wave: all seven p24 rows fixed, plus the p23 audit's role-recovery observation (new row, forced through
test hooks). Decisions: the avatar menu's Mention clears inline code from the run it inserts before linking (content on
the user's behalf keeps the entity); a set is reported back to the chat whenever the read-back differs from what was set
(the hand-kept list is deleted) and formula / pill runs split per occurrence; a code body holds text only (atoms as their
text, render and read-back); the panel counts what the text route sends; ONE line-break normaliser (Core
`normalisedLineBreaks`: CRLF, CR, `U+2029`, NEL) on the set's render, host pastes / drops and (mirrored in ObjC) the
engine's own paste; marked text in a one-line field is flattened through a new host hook (`tgid_markedTextFilter`); the
model keeps a code language as typed and the wire trims it (`chatInputCodeLanguageForSending`; trimming at settle was
rejected because it eats the space between words typed key by key); role recovery is by identity on both halves. The six
p24 `FuzzKnownDefect` entries are retired (none open) and the language-edge per-step check removed (the axis check now
compares languages exactly). Harness fix found by the first unfiltered run: the `chatMention` op's oracle counted mention
text, which a Mention over a RANGE can lower while landing (default seeds 1, 2, 3, 19; edited 1, 3, 18 — false positives
the open known-defect filter had swallowed); over a range it now asks that a mention run holds the name.

- **Sweeps with NO known-defect entries:** default 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[]`, 61-90 `[]`; edited 1-30 `[]`, 31-60 `[]`, 61-90
  `[]`; no `Fatal error`.
- **Target classes (one run):** 45 tests, 0 failures after one test-expectation fix (UIKit's smart paste adds a space
  before the pasted `ps`); `HarnessRoleRecoveryByIdentityTests` neuter-checked (positional record back: the four split
  cases red, the control green; restored, `cmp`-verified).
- **Full `ComposerHarnessTests`:** 853 run, 14 assertion failures in exactly the 13 legacy-by-design tests, no traps;
  `ComposerHarnessUITests` 16/0. Host suites 1466/5 (environmental spelling/underline), 1065/0 (1 skipped), 127/0;
  SwiftPM Core 497/1 (`DeferredConfigurationRegisterTests`, known); `TextFormatTests` 303/0; full app build green.
